===== FILE: app/config.py =====
     1	"""Application settings, loaded from environment variables (prefix TTB_).
     2	
     3	Every limit that a reviewer might probe is here, named, and documented in LIMITS.md.
     4	"""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	import os
     9	from functools import lru_cache
    10	from pathlib import Path
    11	
    12	from pydantic import Field
    13	from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
    14	
    15	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    16	MODELS_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "app" / "models"
    17	STATIC_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "app" / "static"
    18	
    19	
    20	class Settings(BaseSettings):
    21	    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix="TTB_", env_file=".env", extra="ignore")
    22	
    23	    # --- deployment identity
    24	    app_name: str = "Label Check (prototype)"
    25	    agency_name: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Optional agency name for internal branding")
    26	    git_sha: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: os.environ.get("GIT_SHA", "dev"))
    27	    trust_proxy: bool = Field(default=False, description="Trust the first X-Forwarded-For hop for client identity")
    28	
    29	    # --- OCR engine
    30	    ocr_workers: int = Field(default_factory=lambda: max(1, min(8, os.cpu_count() or 1)))
    31	    ocr_intra_op_threads: int = 1
    32	    ocr_det_limit_side_len: int = 640
    33	    ocr_rec_batch_num: int = 16
    34	    ocr_use_cls: bool = False
    35	    ocr_ascii_alphabet: bool = Field(
    36	        default=True,
    37	        description="Decode with the recognizer's alphabet restricted to printable ASCII (English label text)",
    38	    )
    39	    ocr_max_side: int = Field(default=1280, description="Images are downscaled to this longest side before OCR")
    40	    ocr_low_conf_retry: float = Field(default=0.80, description="Mean confidence below which a 90-degree retry runs")
    41	    ocr_min_lines_retry: int = Field(default=3, description="Fewer detected lines than this also triggers the retry")
    42	    models_dir: Path = MODELS_DIR
    43	
    44	    # --- request limits (see LIMITS.md)
    45	    max_image_bytes: int = 10 * 1024 * 1024
    46	    max_request_bytes: int = 40 * 1024 * 1024
    47	    max_image_pixels: int = 25_000_000
    48	    max_images_per_application: int = 6
    49	    max_compare_items: int = 100
    50	    max_csv_rows: int = 5000
    51	    max_csv_bytes: int = 2 * 1024 * 1024
    52	    per_client_inflight: int = 4
    53	    global_inflight: int = Field(default=24, description="Concurrent metered requests across all clients")
    54	    interactive_wait_seconds: float = Field(
    55	        default=8.0, description="How long an interactive request may wait for a worker slot"
    56	    )
    57	
    58	    # --- matching thresholds (tuned against tests/fixtures; see docs/OCR_EVAL.md)
    59	    match_review_threshold: int = Field(
    60	        default=90, description="Fuzzy score at/above which a non-exact match is Needs review"
    61	    )
    62	    match_mismatch_threshold: int = Field(default=70, description="Below this the best candidate counts as Not found")
    63	    warning_mismatch_similarity: float = Field(
    64	        default=0.80, description="Below this the warning is treated as wording differs"
    65	    )
    66	    net_contents_tolerance: float = Field(default=0.01, description="Relative tolerance for unit-conversion rounding")
    67	
    68	    @property
    69	    def static_dir(self) -> Path:
    70	        return STATIC_DIR
    71	
    72	
    73	@lru_cache
    74	def get_settings() -> Settings:
    75	    return Settings()

===== FILE: app/main.py =====
     1	"""FastAPI application factory."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import asyncio
     6	import logging
     7	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable
     8	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     9	
    10	from fastapi import FastAPI
    11	from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
    12	from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    13	from starlette.formparsers import MultiPartParser
    14	
    15	from app import __version__
    16	from app.config import Settings, get_settings
    17	from app.ocr.base import OcrEngine
    18	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool
    19	from app.ocr.rapid import RapidEngine
    20	from app.routes.api import router as api_router
    21	from app.routes.csv_routes import router as csv_router
    22	from app.security import AdmissionLimiter, SecurityMiddleware, install_error_handlers
    23	
    24	logging.basicConfig(
    25	    level=logging.INFO, format='{"ts":"%(asctime)s","level":"%(levelname)s","logger":"%(name)s","msg":"%(message)s"}'
    26	)
    27	log = logging.getLogger("app")
    28	
    29	
    30	def create_app(
    31	    settings: Settings | None = None,
    32	    *,
    33	    warm: bool = True,
    34	    engine_factory: Callable[[Settings], OcrEngine] | None = None,
    35	) -> FastAPI:
    36	    settings = settings or get_settings()
    37	    make_engine = engine_factory or (lambda s: RapidEngine(s))
    38	    # Multipart parts larger than this are spooled to disk by Starlette. Keep every image (per-image cap)
    39	    # in memory so uploads never touch the filesystem; the request-size cap bounds total memory.
    40	    MultiPartParser.spool_max_size = settings.max_image_bytes + 1024 * 1024
    41	
    42	    @asynccontextmanager
    43	    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    44	        pool = OcrPool(settings, lambda: make_engine(settings))
    45	        app.state.pool = pool
    46	        if warm:
    47	            loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    48	            # Warm in the background so health answers immediately with ready=false; verify returns 503 until warm.
    49	            warm_task = loop.run_in_executor(None, pool.warmup)
    50	            warm_task.add_done_callback(
    51	                lambda fut: (
    52	                    log.error("OCR warm-up failed; /ready stays 503: %s", fut.exception())
    53	                    if fut.exception()
    54	                    else log.info("OCR warm-up complete")
    55	                )
    56	            )
    57	            app.state.warm_task = warm_task
    58	        yield
    59	        pool.shutdown()
    60	
    61	    app = FastAPI(
    62	        title="Label Check (prototype)",
    63	        version=__version__,
    64	        description=(
    65	            "Prototype for verifying alcohol beverage label artwork against COLA application data. "
    66	            "The tool recommends; the compliance agent decides. Nothing is stored."
    67	        ),
    68	        lifespan=lifespan,
    69	        docs_url=None,
    70	        redoc_url=None,  # no CDN-hosted docs UI (restricted networks)
    71	        openapi_url="/api/v1/openapi.json",
    72	    )
    73	    app.state.settings = settings
    74	    limiter = AdmissionLimiter(settings)
    75	    app.state.limiter = limiter
    76	    app.add_middleware(SecurityMiddleware, settings=settings, limiter=limiter)
    77	    install_error_handlers(app)
    78	    app.include_router(api_router)
    79	    app.include_router(csv_router)
    80	
    81	    static_dir = settings.static_dir
    82	    if static_dir.exists():
    83	        app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(static_dir)), name="static")
    84	
    85	        @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    86	        async def index() -> FileResponse:
    87	            return FileResponse(static_dir / "index.html", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})
    88	
    89	    return app
    90	
    91	
    92	app = create_app()

===== FILE: app/ocr/alphabet.py =====
     1	"""Alphabet-constrained CTC decoding.
     2	
     3	The multilingual recognizer's dictionary has ~18,700 classes (Latin with diacritics, Cyrillic, CJK,
     4	emoji). On English label artwork it occasionally emits an accented letter for a plain one
     5	("alcoholi\u010d", "driv\u00e9"), which would turn an exact government warning into a "Needs review".
     6	US alcohol labels are English text; the regulation text is pure ASCII. Restricting the decoder to
     7	printable ASCII is therefore a recognizer configuration, applied to every line before the argmax,
     8	not a normalization of the comparison: there is exactly one transcript, and "exact" still means the
     9	transcript equals the required text character for character.
    10	
    11	Consequence, stated in LIMITS.md: a genuinely accented letter on a label (a French back label, a
    12	brand like "Ch\u00e2teau") is read as its base letter. Field comparisons already fold accents, and the
    13	evidence crop shows the print as it is.
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	from typing import Any
    19	
    20	import numpy as np
    21	
    22	
    23	def ascii_mask(characters: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
    24	    """Boolean array, True for classes to suppress. Keeps blank (index 0), space, printable ASCII."""
    25	    suppress = np.ones(len(characters), dtype=bool)
    26	    for i, ch in enumerate(characters):
    27	        if i == 0 or ch == "blank" or ch == " " or (len(ch) == 1 and 32 <= ord(ch) < 127):
    28	            suppress[i] = False
    29	    return suppress
    30	
    31	
    32	class AlphabetConstrainedDecode:
    33	    """Wraps rapidocr's CTCLabelDecode: zeroes disallowed classes, then delegates.
    34	
    35	    Works on probabilities (softmax output, the PP-OCR export) and on logits (negative values):
    36	    disallowed classes get 0 or -1e9 respectively, so the argmax picks the best allowed class.
    37	    CTC blank stays allowed, so padding timesteps still decode to nothing.
    38	    """
    39	
    40	    def __init__(self, inner: Any) -> None:
    41	        self._inner = inner
    42	        self.character = list(inner.character)
    43	        self._suppress = ascii_mask(self.character)
    44	        self.suppressed_classes = int(self._suppress.sum())
    45	
    46	    def __call__(self, preds: np.ndarray, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
    47	        p = np.array(preds, copy=True)
    48	        if p.ndim == 3 and p.shape[2] == self._suppress.shape[0]:
    49	            fill = -1e9 if float(p.min()) < 0.0 else 0.0
    50	            p[..., self._suppress] = fill
    51	        return self._inner(p, *args, **kwargs)
    52	
    53	    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> Any:  # everything else (decode, dict, ...) passes through
    54	        return getattr(self._inner, name)

===== FILE: app/ocr/pool.py =====
     1	"""Worker pool and admission control for OCR.
     2	
     3	One engine per worker thread, one ONNX intra-op thread per engine (measured to scale near
     4	linearly, see docs/OCR_EVAL.md). A single capacity limiter of N slots protects the CPU.
     5	
     6	Admission policy (pinned by tests/unit/test_pool.py):
     7	- Interactive requests may wait briefly for a slot (a person is watching a spinner).
     8	- Batch requests never wait. They are refused with BusyError (HTTP 429 + Retry-After) when every
     9	  slot is busy, or when an interactive request is already waiting, so the next free slot goes to
    10	  the person rather than to the queue. When nobody is waiting, batch may use every slot.
    11	- A slot is held for as long as an OCR thread is actually running. If the awaiting request is
    12	  cancelled (client disconnect), the slot is released when the thread finishes, not before:
    13	  ONNX Runtime inference cannot be interrupted, so pretending the capacity is free would let more
    14	  jobs onto the CPU than there are workers.
    15	- A slot can be held across several images (batch requests use one slot for all their images), so a
    16	  multi-image request is never refused halfway through.
    17	
    18	Health checks never touch the pool, so they answer even when it is saturated.
    19	"""
    20	
    21	from __future__ import annotations
    22	
    23	import asyncio
    24	import logging
    25	import threading
    26	import time
    27	from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Awaitable, Callable
    28	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    29	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
    30	
    31	import numpy as np
    32	
    33	from app.config import Settings
    34	
    35	from .base import OcrEngine, RawLine
    36	
    37	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38	
    39	Runner = Callable[[np.ndarray], Awaitable[list[RawLine]]]
    40	
    41	
    42	class BusyError(Exception):
    43	    def __init__(self, retry_after: int = 1) -> None:
    44	        super().__init__("OCR capacity is fully in use")
    45	        self.retry_after = retry_after
    46	
    47	
    48	class OcrPool:
    49	    def __init__(self, settings: Settings, engine_factory: Callable[[], OcrEngine]) -> None:
    50	        self.workers = max(1, settings.ocr_workers)
    51	        self.interactive_wait_s = settings.interactive_wait_seconds
    52	        self._factory = engine_factory
    53	        self._executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=self.workers, thread_name_prefix="ocr")
    54	        self._local = threading.local()
    55	        self._sem = asyncio.Semaphore(self.workers)
    56	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    57	        self._active = 0  # slots held (running or reserved)
    58	        self._interactive_waiting = 0
    59	        self._info: dict[str, str] = {}
    60	        self.ready = False
    61	        self.error: str | None = None
    62	
    63	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ engines
    64	    def _engine(self) -> OcrEngine:
    65	        eng = getattr(self._local, "engine", None)
    66	        if eng is None:
    67	            eng = self._factory()
    68	            self._local.engine = eng
    69	            self._info = eng.info()
    70	        return eng
    71	
    72	    def _run(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
    73	        return self._engine().recognize(rgb)
    74	
    75	    def warmup(self) -> None:
    76	        """Build one engine per worker thread and run a tiny image through each."""
    77	        blank = np.full((64, 256, 3), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    78	        barrier = threading.Barrier(self.workers)
    79	
    80	        def job() -> None:
    81	            self._engine().recognize(blank)
    82	            barrier.wait(timeout=120)  # hold the thread so every worker gets its own engine
    83	
    84	        try:
    85	            futures = [self._executor.submit(job) for _ in range(self.workers)]
    86	            for f in futures:
    87	                f.result(timeout=180)
    88	        except Exception as exc:  # surfaced through /health and /ready instead of dying silently
    89	            self.error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
    90	            log.exception("OCR pool warm-up failed")
    91	            raise
    92	        self.ready = True
    93	        log.info("OCR pool ready: %d worker(s), %s", self.workers, self._info)
    94	
    95	    def shutdown(self) -> None:
    96	        self._executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
    97	
    98	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ admission
    99	    @property
   100	    def in_flight(self) -> int:
   101	        return self._active
   102	
   103	    @property
   104	    def interactive_waiting(self) -> int:
   105	        return self._interactive_waiting
   106	
   107	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
   108	        return dict(self._info)
   109	
   110	    async def _admit(self, interactive: bool) -> None:
   111	        if interactive:
   112	            with self._lock:
   113	                self._interactive_waiting += 1
   114	            try:
   115	                await asyncio.wait_for(self._sem.acquire(), timeout=self.interactive_wait_s)
   116	            except TimeoutError as exc:
   117	                raise BusyError(retry_after=2) from exc
   118	            finally:
   119	                with self._lock:
   120	                    self._interactive_waiting -= 1
   121	            with self._lock:
   122	                self._active += 1
   123	            return
   124	        with self._lock:
   125	            if self._active >= self.workers or self._interactive_waiting > 0:
   126	                raise BusyError(retry_after=1)
   127	            self._active += 1  # reserve before awaiting so concurrent batch callers cannot all pass
   128	        await self._sem.acquire()  # cannot block: holders + reservations never exceed the slot count
   129	
   130	    def _release(self) -> None:
   131	        with self._lock:
   132	            self._active -= 1
   133	        self._sem.release()
   134	
   135	    @asynccontextmanager
   136	    async def slot(self, *, interactive: bool) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[Runner, int]]:
   137	        """Hold one worker slot. Yields (run, queue_ms); ``await run(rgb)`` executes OCR on the slot.
   138	
   139	        The slot is released when the block exits, or, if the block is cancelled while a thread is
   140	        still running, when that thread finishes.
   141	        """
   142	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   143	        await self._admit(interactive)
   144	        queue_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
   145	        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
   146	        pending: list[asyncio.Future[list[RawLine]]] = []
   147	
   148	        async def run(rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
   149	            fut = loop.run_in_executor(self._executor, self._run, rgb)
   150	            pending.append(fut)
   151	            try:
   152	                return await asyncio.shield(fut)
   153	            finally:
   154	                if fut.done():
   155	                    pending.remove(fut)
   156	
   157	        try:
   158	            yield run, queue_ms
   159	        finally:
   160	            running = [f for f in pending if not f.done()]
   161	            if running:
   162	                running[-1].add_done_callback(lambda _f: self._release())
   163	            else:
   164	                self._release()
   165	
   166	    async def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray, *, interactive: bool) -> tuple[list[RawLine], int, int]:
   167	        """Run OCR on one image in its own slot. Returns (lines, queue_ms, ocr_ms)."""
   168	        async with self.slot(interactive=interactive) as (run, queue_ms):
   169	            t1 = time.perf_counter()
   170	            lines = await run(rgb)
   171	            return lines, queue_ms, int((time.perf_counter() - t1) * 1000)

===== FILE: app/ocr/rapid.py =====
     1	"""RapidOCR (ONNX Runtime) engine with vendored models and explicit thread settings.
     2	
     3	Model files come only from ``settings.models_dir`` (see tools/vendor_models.py and
     4	app/models/MANIFEST.json). Nothing is downloaded.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import logging
    11	from pathlib import Path
    12	from typing import Any
    13	
    14	import numpy as np
    15	
    16	from app.config import Settings
    17	
    18	from .alphabet import AlphabetConstrainedDecode
    19	from .base import RawLine
    20	
    21	log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22	
    23	
    24	class RapidEngine:
    25	    name = "rapidocr-onnxruntime"
    26	
    27	    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
    28	        from rapidocr import RapidOCR
    29	
    30	        manifest = json.loads((settings.models_dir / "MANIFEST.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    31	        models = {role: settings.models_dir / m["file"] for role, m in manifest["models"].items()}
    32	        for role, path in models.items():
    33	            if not Path(path).exists():
    34	                raise FileNotFoundError(f"vendored OCR model missing: {role} -> {path}")
    35	        params = {
    36	            "Global.use_cls": settings.ocr_use_cls,
    37	            "Global.log_level": "warning",
    38	            "Det.model_path": str(models["det"]),
    39	            "Rec.model_path": str(models["rec"]),
    40	            "Det.limit_side_len": settings.ocr_det_limit_side_len,
    41	            "Rec.rec_batch_num": settings.ocr_rec_batch_num,
    42	            "EngineConfig.onnxruntime.intra_op_num_threads": settings.ocr_intra_op_threads,
    43	            "EngineConfig.onnxruntime.inter_op_num_threads": 1,
    44	        }
    45	        if "cls" in models:
    46	            params["Cls.model_path"] = str(models["cls"])
    47	        self._ocr = RapidOCR(params=params)
    48	        self.alphabet = "full"
    49	        if settings.ocr_ascii_alphabet:
    50	            rec: Any = self._ocr.text_rec  # the library types this as CTCLabelDecode; we wrap it
    51	            rec.postprocess_op = AlphabetConstrainedDecode(rec.postprocess_op)
    52	            dec = rec.postprocess_op
    53	            self.alphabet = f"printable ASCII ({dec.suppressed_classes} of {len(dec.character)} classes suppressed)"
    54	        self._models = {role: f"{m['file']} (sha256 {m['sha256'][:12]})" for role, m in manifest["models"].items()}
    55	        try:
    56	            from importlib.metadata import version
    57	
    58	            self._version = f"rapidocr {version('rapidocr')} / onnxruntime {version('onnxruntime')}"
    59	        except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - metadata missing in odd environments
    60	            self._version = "rapidocr"
    61	
    62	    def recognize(self, rgb: np.ndarray) -> list[RawLine]:
    63	        bgr = np.ascontiguousarray(rgb[:, :, ::-1])
    64	        result: Any = self._ocr(bgr)
    65	        boxes, txts, scores = (
    66	            getattr(result, "boxes", None),
    67	            getattr(result, "txts", None),
    68	            getattr(result, "scores", None),
    69	        )
    70	        if boxes is None or not txts:
    71	            return []
    72	        lines: list[RawLine] = []
    73	        confs = list(scores) if scores is not None else [0.0] * len(txts)
    74	        for box, text, score in zip(boxes, txts, confs, strict=True):
    75	            if len(box) != 4 or not text or not str(text).strip():
    76	                continue
    77	            quad = (
    78	                (float(box[0][0]), float(box[0][1])),
    79	                (float(box[1][0]), float(box[1][1])),
    80	                (float(box[2][0]), float(box[2][1])),
    81	                (float(box[3][0]), float(box[3][1])),
    82	            )
    83	            lines.append(RawLine(text=str(text).strip(), confidence=float(score), box=quad))
    84	        return lines
    85	
    86	    def info(self) -> dict[str, str]:
    87	        return {"engine": self._version, "alphabet": self.alphabet, **self._models}

===== FILE: app/pipeline/compare.py =====
     1	"""Compare application data with OCR lines and produce checks, a warning report and a verdict.
     2	
     3	Pure function: no I/O, deterministic for the same inputs.
     4	"""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	import re
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from app.config import Settings
    12	from app.schemas import (
    13	    ApplicationFields,
    14	    BeverageType,
    15	    Check,
    16	    CompareResult,
    17	    Evidence,
    18	    ImageInfo,
    19	    OcrLine,
    20	    Status,
    21	    Verdict,
    22	    WarningReport,
    23	)
    24	
    25	from .match import best_span, status_for
    26	from .parsers import (
    27	    Alcohol,
    28	    alcohol_matches,
    29	    alcohol_statement_required,
    30	    fill_rule,
    31	    is_standard_fill,
    32	    parse_alcohol,
    33	    parse_volumes,
    34	    volumes_match,
    35	)
    36	from .warning import build_report
    37	
    38	_LABELS = {
    39	    "brand_name": "Brand name",
    40	    "class_type": "Class / type designation",
    41	    "alcohol_content": "Alcohol content",
    42	    "net_contents": "Net contents",
    43	    "bottler": "Name and address of bottler / producer",
    44	    "country_of_origin": "Country of origin",
    45	}
    46	
    47	
    48	_ORIGIN_PREFIX = re.compile(
    49	    r"^\s*(?:product of|produce of|made in|produced in|distilled in|bottled in|imported from|country of origin:?)\s+",
    50	    re.I,
    51	)
    52	
    53	
    54	def _text_check(check_id: str, expected: str, lines: list[OcrLine], s: Settings, *, rule: str | None = None) -> Check:
    55	    cand = best_span(expected, lines)
    56	    status, note = status_for(
    57	        cand, expected, review_at=s.match_review_threshold, mismatch_at=s.match_mismatch_threshold
    58	    )
    59	    return Check(
    60	        id=check_id,
    61	        label=_LABELS[check_id],
    62	        status=status,
    63	        expected=expected,
    64	        found=cand.text if cand else None,
    65	        score=float(cand.score) if cand else None,
    66	        note=note,
    67	        rule=rule,
    68	        evidence=cand.evidence if cand else [],
    69	    )
    70	
    71	
    72	def _origin_check(expected: str, lines: list[OcrLine], s: Settings) -> Check:
    73	    """Country of origin: 'USA' in the application matches 'Product of USA' on the label."""
    74	    stripped = [ln.model_copy(update={"text": _ORIGIN_PREFIX.sub("", ln.text)}) for ln in lines]
    75	    check = _text_check("country_of_origin", expected, stripped, s, rule="27 CFR 5.69 / 4.35 / 7.69; 19 CFR 134")
    76	    if check.evidence:
    77	        # restore the full line text in the evidence and the 'found' value for display
    78	        found_lines = [
    79	            ln for ln in lines if any(ln.box == ev.box and ln.image_index == ev.image_index for ev in check.evidence)
    80	        ]
    81	        check.evidence = [Evidence(image_index=ln.image_index, box=ln.box, text=ln.text) for ln in found_lines]
    82	        if found_lines:
    83	            check.found = " ".join(ln.text for ln in found_lines)
    84	            if check.status is Status.match and check.found != expected:
    85	                check.note = f"Label says '{check.found}'."
    86	    return check
    87	
    88	
    89	def _alcohol_check(app: ApplicationFields, lines: list[OcrLine], s: Settings) -> Check:
    90	    required, why = alcohol_statement_required(app.beverage_type, app.class_type)
    91	    expected = parse_alcohol(app.alcohol_content, allow_bare=True) if app.alcohol_content else None
    92	    # Parse every line; prefer the statement that agrees with the application, else the first one.
    93	    candidates: list[tuple[Alcohol, OcrLine]] = []
    94	    for ln in lines:
    95	        got = parse_alcohol(ln.text)
    96	        if got:
    97	            candidates.append((got, ln))
    98	    found: Alcohol | None = None
    99	    ev: list[Evidence] = []
   100	    distinct = sorted({round(a.percent, 1) for a, _ in candidates if a.percent is not None})
   101	    if candidates:
   102	        chosen = next(((a, ln) for a, ln in candidates if expected and alcohol_matches(expected, a)), candidates[0])
   103	        found, _line = chosen
   104	        ev = [Evidence(image_index=ln.image_index, box=ln.box, text=ln.text) for _, ln in candidates]
   105	    if found is None:  # statements sometimes wrap ("45% Alc./Vol." / "(90 Proof)")
   106	        found = parse_alcohol(" ".join(ln.text for ln in lines))
   107	    base: dict[str, Any] = {
   108	        "id": "alcohol_content",
   109	        "label": _LABELS["alcohol_content"],
   110	        "expected": app.alcohol_content,
   111	        "found": found.raw if found and ev else (f"{found.percent}%" if found else None),
   112	        "evidence": ev,
   113	        "rule": "27 CFR 5.65 / 4.36 / 7.65",
   114	    }
   115	    if expected is None:
   116	        if found is None:
   117	            st = Status.not_found if required else Status.info
   118	            return Check(status=st, note="No alcohol statement in the application or on the label. " + why, **base)
   119	        if required:
   120	            return Check(
   121	                status=Status.needs_review,
   122	                note=f"The application gives no alcohol content, but one is required and the label states "
   123	                f"{found.percent}%. Confirm the application. " + why,
   124	                **base,
   125	            )
   126	        return Check(
   127	            status=Status.info,
   128	            note=f"Application gives no alcohol content; the label states {found.percent}%. " + why,
   129	            **base,
   130	        )
   131	    if found is None:
   132	        return Check(
   133	            status=Status.not_found, note="No alcohol content statement could be read on the label. " + why, **base
   134	        )
   135	    if len(distinct) > 1:
   136	        stated = ", ".join(f"{v:g}%" for v in distinct)
   137	        return Check(
   138	            status=Status.mismatch,
   139	            score=0.0,
   140	            note=f"The label images state different alcohol contents ({stated}); the application says "
   141	            f"{expected.percent:g}%. A label may not contradict itself.",
   142	            **base,
   143	        )
   144	    if alcohol_matches(expected, found):
   145	        note = f"Both state {found.percent}% alcohol by volume."
   146	        if expected.proof is not None and found.proof is not None and abs(expected.proof - found.proof) > 0.2:
   147	            return Check(
   148	                status=Status.mismatch,
   149	                score=0.0,
   150	                note=f"Percentages agree but proof differs: application {expected.proof:g}, label {found.proof:g}.",
   151	                **base,
   152	            )
   153	        if found.consistent is False:
   154	            note += f" The label's proof ({found.proof:g}) does not equal twice the percentage. Confirm on the image."
   155	            return Check(status=Status.needs_review, score=100.0, note=note, **base)
   156	        return Check(status=Status.match, score=100.0, note=note, **base)
   157	    return Check(
   158	        status=Status.mismatch,
   159	        score=0.0,
   160	        note=f"Application states {expected.percent}%; the label reads {found.percent}%.",
   161	        **base,
   162	    )
   163	
   164	
   165	def _net_contents_check(app: ApplicationFields, lines: list[OcrLine], s: Settings) -> tuple[Check, Check | None]:
   166	    expected = parse_volumes(app.net_contents)
   167	    base: dict[str, Any] = {
   168	        "id": "net_contents",
   169	        "label": _LABELS["net_contents"],
   170	        "expected": app.net_contents,
   171	        "rule": "27 CFR 5.70 / 4.37 / 7.70",
   172	    }
   173	    if not expected:
   174	        return Check(
   175	            status=Status.not_checked, note="Could not interpret the application's net contents value.", **base
   176	        ), None
   177	    exp_ml = expected[0].ml
   178	    best_line: OcrLine | None = None
   179	    best_vol = None
   180	    for ln in lines:
   181	        for v in parse_volumes(ln.text):
   182	            if volumes_match(exp_ml, v.ml, tolerance=s.net_contents_tolerance):
   183	                best_line, best_vol = ln, v
   184	                break
   185	            if best_vol is None:
   186	                best_line, best_vol = ln, v
   187	        if best_vol and volumes_match(exp_ml, best_vol.ml, tolerance=s.net_contents_tolerance):
   188	            break
   189	    fill_check: Check | None = None
   190	    std = is_standard_fill(app.beverage_type, exp_ml)
   191	    if std is False:
   192	        fill_check = Check(
   193	            id="standard_of_fill",
   194	            label="Authorized container size",
   195	            status=Status.needs_review,
   196	            expected=app.net_contents,
   197	            found=None,
   198	            note=(
   199	                f"{exp_ml:g} mL is not on the authorized standards of fill list for "
   200	                f"{'distilled spirits' if app.beverage_type is BeverageType.spirits else 'wine'} "
   201	                f"(as amended January 2025). Verify before approval."
   202	            ),
   203	            rule=fill_rule(app.beverage_type),
   204	        )
   205	    if best_vol is None:
   206	        return Check(
   207	            status=Status.not_found, note="No net contents statement could be read on the label.", **base
   208	        ), fill_check
   209	    ev = [Evidence(image_index=best_line.image_index, box=best_line.box, text=best_line.text)] if best_line else []
   210	    if volumes_match(exp_ml, best_vol.ml, tolerance=s.net_contents_tolerance):
   211	        return Check(
   212	            status=Status.match,
   213	            found=best_vol.raw,
   214	            score=100.0,
   215	            evidence=ev,
   216	            note=f"Both state {exp_ml:g} mL"
   217	            + (f" ({best_vol.raw} on the label)." if best_vol.unit not in ("ml",) else "."),
   218	            **base,
   219	        ), fill_check
   220	    return Check(
   221	        status=Status.mismatch,
   222	        found=best_vol.raw,
   223	        score=0.0,
   224	        evidence=ev,
   225	        note=f"Application states {exp_ml:g} mL; the label reads {best_vol.raw} ({best_vol.ml:g} mL).",
   226	        **base,
   227	    ), fill_check
   228	
   229	
   230	def _verdict(checks: list[Check], warning: WarningReport, images: list[ImageInfo], s: Settings) -> tuple[Verdict, str]:
   231	    if images and all(not im.quality.readable for im in images):
   232	        return Verdict.unreadable, "None of the images could be read reliably. Request clearer label images."
   233	    hard = {Status.mismatch, Status.not_found}
   234	    soft = {Status.needs_review}
   235	    statuses = [c.status for c in checks if c.id != "standard_of_fill"]
   236	    issues = [c for c in checks if c.status in hard]
   237	    reviews = [c for c in checks if c.status in soft]
   238	    if warning.assessment == "not_required":
   239	        pass
   240	    elif warning.assessment in ("absent", "wording"):
   241	        issues.append(Check(id="warning", label="Government warning", status=Status.mismatch))
   242	    elif warning.assessment in ("case", "noise") or warning.anchor_caps is Status.needs_review:
   243	        reviews.append(Check(id="warning", label="Government warning", status=Status.needs_review))
   244	    if issues:
   245	        names = ", ".join(c.label.lower() for c in issues)
   246	        also = ", ".join(c.label.lower() for c in reviews if c.id not in {i.id for i in issues})
   247	        tail = f" Also confirm: {also}." if also else ""
   248	        return Verdict.issues_found, f"Issues found: {names}.{tail} The agent should review before any decision."
   249	    if reviews:
   250	        names = ", ".join(c.label.lower() for c in reviews)
   251	        return Verdict.needs_review, f"Everything else matches; please confirm: {names}."
   252	    if all(st in {Status.match, Status.info, Status.not_checked} for st in statuses) and (
   253	        warning.exact or warning.assessment == "not_required"
   254	    ):
   255	        return Verdict.ready_for_approval, "All checks match and the warning is exact. Ready for the agent's approval."
   256	    return Verdict.needs_review, "Some checks could not be completed. Review the items marked."
   257	
   258	
   259	def compare(app: ApplicationFields, lines: list[OcrLine], images: list[ImageInfo], s: Settings) -> CompareResult:
   260	    checks: list[Check] = [
   261	        _text_check("brand_name", app.brand_name, lines, s, rule="27 CFR 5.64 / 4.33 / 7.64"),
   262	        _text_check("class_type", app.class_type, lines, s, rule="27 CFR 5.63 / 4.34 / 7.63"),
   263	        _alcohol_check(app, lines, s),
   264	    ]
   265	    net, fill = _net_contents_check(app, lines, s)
   266	    checks.append(net)
   267	    if app.bottler:
   268	        checks.append(_text_check("bottler", app.bottler, lines, s, rule="27 CFR 5.66 / 4.35 / 7.66"))
   269	    else:
   270	        checks.append(
   271	            Check(
   272	                id="bottler",
   273	                label=_LABELS["bottler"],
   274	                status=Status.not_checked,
   275	                note="Not compared: the application did not provide the bottler's name and address.",
   276	                rule="27 CFR 5.66 / 4.35 / 7.66",
   277	            )
   278	        )
   279	    if app.country_of_origin:
   280	        checks.append(_origin_check(app.country_of_origin, lines, s))
   281	    elif app.imported:
   282	        checks.append(
   283	            Check(
   284	                id="country_of_origin",
   285	                label=_LABELS["country_of_origin"],
   286	                status=Status.not_checked,
   287	                note="Marked as imported but the application gives no country of origin to compare. "
   288	                "Imports must state the country of origin.",
   289	                rule="19 CFR 134",
   290	            )
   291	        )
   292	    else:
   293	        checks.append(
   294	            Check(
   295	                id="country_of_origin",
   296	                label=_LABELS["country_of_origin"],
   297	                status=Status.not_checked,
   298	                note="Not compared: the application did not provide a country of origin (required for imports).",
   299	                rule="19 CFR 134",
   300	            )
   301	        )
   302	    if fill:
   303	        checks.append(fill)
   304	    warning = build_report(lines, mismatch_similarity=s.warning_mismatch_similarity)
   305	    stated = parse_alcohol(app.alcohol_content, allow_bare=True) if app.alcohol_content else None
   306	    if stated and stated.percent is not None and stated.percent < 0.5:
   307	        warning = warning.model_copy(
   308	            update={
   309	                "assessment": "not_required",
   310	                "notes": [
   311	                    f"The application states {stated.percent:g}% alcohol. The health warning statement is required "
   312	                    "only for beverages of 0.5% alcohol or more (27 CFR 16.10), so its absence is not a finding."
   313	                ]
   314	                + ([] if not warning.present else ["A warning statement is present anyway."]),
   315	            }
   316	        )
   317	    verdict, summary = _verdict(checks, warning, images, s)
   318	    return CompareResult(verdict=verdict, checks=checks, warning=warning, summary=summary)

===== FILE: app/pipeline/images.py =====
     1	"""Image intake: format sniffing, decompression-bomb guard, EXIF orientation, downscaling, and
     2	the coordinate transforms that map OCR boxes back to the oriented original image.
     3	
     4	Canonical space = the image after EXIF transpose, at its original resolution. Every box the API
     5	returns lives there.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	from collections.abc import Sequence
    11	from dataclasses import dataclass
    12	from io import BytesIO
    13	
    14	import numpy as np
    15	from PIL import Image, ImageOps
    16	
    17	from app.schemas import Quad
    18	
    19	_SIGNATURES: tuple[tuple[bytes, str], ...] = (
    20	    (b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n", "png"),
    21	    (b"\xff\xd8\xff", "jpeg"),
    22	    (b"GIF87a", "gif"),
    23	    (b"GIF89a", "gif"),
    24	    (b"II*\x00", "tiff"),
    25	    (b"MM\x00*", "tiff"),
    26	    (b"BM", "bmp"),
    27	)
    28	SUPPORTED = ("png", "jpeg", "gif", "webp", "tiff", "bmp")
    29	
    30	
    31	class ImageError(Exception):
    32	    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, hint: str | None = None) -> None:
    33	        super().__init__(message)
    34	        self.code, self.message, self.hint = code, message, hint
    35	
    36	
    37	def sniff_format(data: bytes) -> str | None:
    38	    for sig, fmt in _SIGNATURES:
    39	        if data.startswith(sig):
    40	            return fmt
    41	    if data[:4] == b"RIFF" and data[8:12] == b"WEBP":
    42	        return "webp"
    43	    return None
    44	
    45	
    46	@dataclass
    47	class DecodedImage:
    48	    array: np.ndarray  # RGB uint8, downscaled working copy
    49	    width: int  # canonical (oriented original) width
    50	    height: int  # canonical height
    51	    scale: float  # working = canonical * scale
    52	    format: str
    53	    filename: str | None
    54	
    55	
    56	def decode_image(data: bytes, *, max_pixels: int, max_side: int, filename: str | None = None) -> DecodedImage:
    57	    fmt = sniff_format(data)
    58	    if fmt is None:
    59	        head = data[:5]
    60	        if head.startswith(b"%PDF"):
    61	            raise ImageError(
    62	                "unsupported_format",
    63	                "PDF files are not supported.",
    64	                "Export the label artwork as PNG or JPEG and upload that.",
    65	            )
    66	        if head.lstrip().startswith(b"<"):
    67	            raise ImageError(
    68	                "unsupported_format",
    69	                "SVG and other text-based files are not supported.",
    70	                "Upload PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, TIFF or BMP.",
    71	            )
    72	        if data[4:12] in (b"ftypheic", b"ftypheix", b"ftypmif1", b"ftypmsf1"):
    73	            raise ImageError(
    74	                "unsupported_format",
    75	                "HEIC photos are not supported.",
    76	                "On iPhone, share the photo as JPEG, or set Camera > Formats to Most Compatible.",
    77	            )
    78	        raise ImageError(
    79	            "unsupported_format", "This file is not a supported image.", "Upload PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, TIFF or BMP."
    80	        )
    81	    try:
    82	        im = Image.open(BytesIO(data))
    83	        w, h = im.size
    84	        if w * h > max_pixels:
    85	            raise ImageError(
    86	                "image_too_large",
    87	                f"This image is {w} x {h} pixels, above the {max_pixels // 1_000_000} megapixel limit.",
    88	                "Resize the image below the limit and upload it again.",
    89	            )
    90	        if fmt == "gif":
    91	            im.seek(0)
    92	        if fmt == "jpeg" and max(w, h) > 2 * max_side:
    93	            im.draft("RGB", (max_side * 2, max_side * 2))  # decode large JPEGs at reduced size: far less memory
    94	        im = ImageOps.exif_transpose(im) or im
    95	        im = im.convert("RGB")
    96	    except ImageError:
    97	        raise
    98	    except Exception as exc:  # Pillow raises many types for corrupt data
    99	        raise ImageError(
   100	            "corrupt_image",
   101	            "This image could not be decoded.",
   102	            "The file may be truncated or corrupt. Re-export it and try again.",
   103	        ) from exc
   104	    cw, ch = im.size
   105	    scale = min(1.0, max_side / max(cw, ch))
   106	    if scale < 1.0:
   107	        im = im.resize((max(1, round(cw * scale)), max(1, round(ch * scale))), Image.LANCZOS)
   108	    return DecodedImage(array=np.asarray(im), width=cw, height=ch, scale=scale, format=fmt, filename=filename)
   109	
   110	
   111	def rotate_array(arr: np.ndarray, degrees: int) -> np.ndarray:
   112	    """Rotate counter-clockwise by 90, 180 or 270 degrees (numpy convention)."""
   113	    k = {0: 0, 90: 1, 180: 2, 270: 3}[degrees]
   114	    return np.ascontiguousarray(np.rot90(arr, k)) if k else arr
   115	
   116	
   117	def unrotate_point(x: float, y: float, degrees: int, rot_w: int, rot_h: int) -> tuple[float, float]:
   118	    """Map a point in a rotated array back to the unrotated array's coordinates.
   119	
   120	    rot_w, rot_h are the rotated array's width and height. Derived from numpy.rot90 semantics:
   121	    k=1 (90 CCW): rotated (x, y) came from original (rot_h - y, x)
   122	    k=2 (180):    original (rot_w - x, rot_h - y)
   123	    k=3 (270 CCW = 90 CW): original (y, rot_w - x)
   124	    """
   125	    if degrees == 0:
   126	        return x, y
   127	    if degrees == 90:
   128	        return rot_h - y, x
   129	    if degrees == 180:
   130	        return rot_w - x, rot_h - y
   131	    if degrees == 270:
   132	        return y, rot_w - x
   133	    raise ValueError(degrees)
   134	
   135	
   136	def to_canonical(
   137	    box: np.ndarray | Sequence[Sequence[float]], *, scale: float, degrees: int, rot_w: int, rot_h: int
   138	) -> Quad:
   139	    """OCR box (4 points, in the working array as fed to OCR, possibly rotated) -> canonical quad."""
   140	    pts = []
   141	    for x, y in box:
   142	        ux, uy = unrotate_point(float(x), float(y), degrees, rot_w, rot_h)
   143	        pts.append((round(ux / scale, 1), round(uy / scale, 1)))
   144	    return (pts[0], pts[1], pts[2], pts[3])

===== FILE: app/pipeline/parsers.py =====
     1	"""Parsers for the two numeric label fields, plus the beverage-type rules that depend on them.
     2	
     3	The same parser runs on the application value and on the OCR text, so "45% Alc./Vol. (90 Proof)"
     4	in the application and "45% ALC/VOL" on the label compare as numbers, not strings.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import re
    10	from dataclasses import dataclass
    11	
    12	from app.schemas import BeverageType
    13	
    14	from .normalize import fold, fold_digits
    15	
    16	_NUM = r"(\d{1,3}(?:[.,]\d{1,3})?)"
    17	
    18	
    19	def _to_float(num: str) -> float:
    20	    """'13,5' -> 13.5 ; '1,750' -> 1750 ; '0.75' -> 0.75"""
    21	    if "," in num and "." not in num:
    22	        head, tail = num.split(",", 1)
    23	        if len(tail) == 3 and head.isdigit():
    24	            return float(head + tail)
    25	        return float(head + "." + tail)
    26	    return float(num.replace(",", ""))
    27	
    28	
    29	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- alcohol content
    30	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    31	class Alcohol:
    32	    percent: float | None
    33	    proof: float | None
    34	    raw: str
    35	
    36	    @property
    37	    def consistent(self) -> bool | None:
    38	        """True when both percent and proof are present and agree (proof = 2 x percent)."""
    39	        if self.percent is None or self.proof is None:
    40	            return None
    41	        return abs(self.proof - 2 * self.percent) <= 0.2
    42	
    43	
    44	_ABV_PATTERNS = [
    45	    # 45% Alc./Vol.   5.2% ALC/VOL   14.1% Alc. by Vol.   45 % abv
    46	    re.compile(_NUM + r"\s*%\s*(?:alc\.?\s*/\s*vol|alc\.?\s*by\s*vol|alc\.?\s*vol|alcohol\s*by\s*vol|abv|vol)", re.I),
    47	    # Alc. 40% by Vol.   ABV 5.2%   Alcohol 13.5%   ALC 13.5% VOL
    48	    re.compile(r"(?:alc(?:ohol)?\.?|abv)[\s.:]*" + _NUM + r"\s*%", re.I),
    49	    # Alcohol 13.5 percent by volume / alc 13.5 by vol (no % sign)
    50	    re.compile(r"(?:alc(?:ohol)?\.?)[\s.:]*" + _NUM + r"\s*(?:%|percent)?\s*(?:alc\.?\s*)?by\s*vol", re.I),
    51	    # 13.5% by volume
    52	    re.compile(_NUM + r"\s*%\s*(?:alc\.?\s*)?by\s*vol", re.I),
    53	]
    54	_PROOF = re.compile(r"(\d{2,3}(?:[.,]\d)?)\s*(?:°\s*)?proof", re.I)
    55	_BARE = re.compile(r"^\s*" + _NUM + r"\s*%?\s*$")
    56	
    57	
    58	def parse_alcohol(text: str, *, allow_bare: bool = False) -> Alcohol | None:
    59	    """Extract alcohol content. ``allow_bare`` accepts a lone number such as "45" (application input only)."""
    60	    t = fold_digits(text)
    61	    percent: float | None = None
    62	    for pat in _ABV_PATTERNS:
    63	        m = pat.search(t)
    64	        if m:
    65	            val = _to_float(m.group(1))
    66	            if 0 < val <= 100:
    67	                percent = val
    68	                break
    69	    proof: float | None = None
    70	    pm = _PROOF.search(t)
    71	    if pm:
    72	        pv = _to_float(pm.group(1))
    73	        if 0 < pv <= 200:
    74	            proof = pv
    75	    if percent is None and proof is not None:
    76	        percent = round(proof / 2, 2)
    77	    if percent is None and allow_bare:
    78	        bm = _BARE.match(t)
    79	        if bm:
    80	            val = _to_float(bm.group(1))
    81	            if 0 < val <= 100:
    82	                percent = val
    83	    if percent is None and proof is None:
    84	        return None
    85	    return Alcohol(percent=percent, proof=proof, raw=text.strip())
    86	
    87	
    88	def alcohol_matches(app: Alcohol, label: Alcohol, *, tolerance: float = 0.05) -> bool:
    89	    if app.percent is None or label.percent is None:
    90	        return False
    91	    return abs(app.percent - label.percent) <= tolerance
    92	
    93	
    94	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- net contents
    95	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    96	class Volume:
    97	    ml: float
    98	    raw: str
    99	    unit: str
   100	
   101	
   102	_UNIT_ML: dict[str, float] = {
   103	    "ml": 1.0,
   104	    "milliliter": 1.0,
   105	    "millilitre": 1.0,
   106	    "cl": 10.0,
   107	    "centiliter": 10.0,
   108	    "centilitre": 10.0,
   109	    "l": 1000.0,
   110	    "liter": 1000.0,
   111	    "litre": 1000.0,
   112	    "ltr": 1000.0,
   113	    "floz": 29.5735295625,
   114	    "oz": 29.5735295625,
   115	    "ounce": 29.5735295625,
   116	    "fluidounce": 29.5735295625,
   117	    "pt": 473.176473,
   118	    "pint": 473.176473,
   119	    "qt": 946.352946,
   120	    "quart": 946.352946,
   121	    "gal": 3785.411784,
   122	    "gallon": 3785.411784,
   123	}
   124	_NET = re.compile(
   125	    r"(\d{1,4}(?:[.,]\d{1,3})?)\s*"
   126	    r"(fl\.?\s*oz\.?|fluid\s*ounces?|ounces?|oz\.?|milliliters?|millilitres?|ml|"
   127	    r"centiliters?|centilitres?|cl|liters?|litres?|ltr|l|pints?|pt\.?|quarts?|qt\.?|gallons?|gal\.?)"
   128	    r"(?![a-z])",
   129	    re.I,
   130	)
   131	
   132	
   133	def _unit_key(unit: str) -> str:
   134	    u = re.sub(r"[^a-z]", "", unit.lower())
   135	    if u.startswith("fl") and u.endswith("oz"):
   136	        return "floz"
   137	    if u.startswith("fluidounce"):
   138	        return "fluidounce"
   139	    for k in (
   140	        "milliliter",
   141	        "millilitre",
   142	        "centiliter",
   143	        "centilitre",
   144	        "liter",
   145	        "litre",
   146	        "gallon",
   147	        "quart",
   148	        "pint",
   149	        "ounce",
   150	    ):
   151	        if u.startswith(k):
   152	            return k
   153	    return u
   154	
   155	
   156	def parse_volumes(text: str) -> list[Volume]:
   157	    """All volume statements in the text, converted to mL. '12 FL OZ (355 mL)' yields two."""
   158	    out: list[Volume] = []
   159	    for m in _NET.finditer(fold_digits(text)):
   160	        unit = _unit_key(m.group(2))
   161	        if unit not in _UNIT_ML:
   162	            continue
   163	        ml = _to_float(m.group(1)) * _UNIT_ML[unit]
   164	        if 0 < ml <= 60_000:
   165	            out.append(Volume(ml=round(ml, 2), raw=m.group(0).strip(), unit=unit))
   166	    return out
   167	
   168	
   169	def volumes_match(app_ml: float, label_ml: float, *, tolerance: float = 0.01) -> bool:
   170	    return abs(app_ml - label_ml) <= max(tolerance * app_ml, 0.5)
   171	
   172	
   173	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- standards of fill
   174	# 27 CFR 5.203(a) as amended Jan 10, 2025 and 4.72(a) as amended Jan 20, 2025 (docs/REGULATIONS.md).
   175	SPIRITS_FILL_ML = (
   176	    3750,
   177	    3000,
   178	    2000,
   179	    1800,
   180	    1750,
   181	    1500,
   182	    1000,
   183	    945,
   184	    900,
   185	    750,
   186	    720,
   187	    710,
   188	    700,
   189	    570,
   190	    500,
   191	    475,
   192	    375,
   193	    355,
   194	    350,
   195	    331,
   196	    250,
   197	    200,
   198	    187,
   199	    100,
   200	    50,
   201	)
   202	WINE_FILL_ML = (
   203	    3000,
   204	    2250,
   205	    1800,
   206	    1500,
   207	    1000,
   208	    750,
   209	    720,
   210	    700,
   211	    620,
   212	    600,
   213	    568,
   214	    550,
   215	    500,
   216	    473,
   217	    375,
   218	    360,
   219	    355,
   220	    330,
   221	    300,
   222	    250,
   223	    200,
   224	    187,
   225	    180,
   226	    100,
   227	    50,
   228	)
   229	
   230	
   231	def is_standard_fill(beverage: BeverageType, ml: float) -> bool | None:
   232	    """True/False for spirits and wine; None for malt beverages (no federal standards of fill)."""
   233	    if beverage is BeverageType.malt:
   234	        return None
   235	    sizes = SPIRITS_FILL_ML if beverage is BeverageType.spirits else WINE_FILL_ML
   236	    if any(abs(ml - s) <= max(0.005 * s, 0.5) for s in sizes):
   237	        return True
   238	    # 4.72(b): wine of 4 L and larger is allowed in even liters
   239	    return beverage is BeverageType.wine and ml >= 4000 and abs(ml / 1000 - round(ml / 1000)) < 0.005
   240	
   241	
   242	def fill_rule(beverage: BeverageType) -> str:
   243	    return "27 CFR 5.203" if beverage is BeverageType.spirits else "27 CFR 4.72"
   244	
   245	
   246	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- beverage rules
   247	def alcohol_statement_required(beverage: BeverageType, class_type: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   248	    """Whether a numeric alcohol statement is federally required, with the reason (simplified)."""
   249	    if beverage is BeverageType.spirits:
   250	        return True, "Required for distilled spirits (27 CFR 5.65)."
   251	    if beverage is BeverageType.wine:
   252	        ct = fold(class_type)
   253	        if "table wine" in ct or "light wine" in ct:
   254	            return False, "Wine of 7-14% may state 'Table Wine' or 'Light Wine' instead of a number (27 CFR 4.36)."
   255	        return True, "Required for wine unless designated Table Wine or Light Wine (27 CFR 4.36)."
   256	    return False, (
   257	        "Optional for malt beverages under federal rules unless alcohol comes from added flavors "
   258	        "(27 CFR 7.63, 7.65); state law may require it."
   259	    )

===== FILE: app/pipeline/warning.py =====
     1	"""Government health warning statement checks (27 CFR Part 16).
     2	
     3	Exactness is literal here: the only Pass is a character-for-character match (after collapsing
     4	whitespace and unifying typographic quotes, which are rendering choices rather than wording).
     5	Anything else is Needs review or a mismatch, never a Pass. The generic normalizer is not used.
     6	"""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import difflib
    11	import re
    12	import unicodedata
    13	from dataclasses import dataclass
    14	
    15	from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    16	from rapidfuzz.distance import Levenshtein
    17	
    18	from app.schemas import Evidence, OcrLine, Status, WarningReport
    19	
    20	from .match import reading_order
    21	from .normalize import collapse_ws, fold, join_hyphenated, unify_punctuation
    22	
    23	# Verbatim text of 27 CFR 16.21, verified against the eCFR API on 2026-09-03 (docs/REGULATIONS.md).
    24	CANONICAL = (
    25	    "GOVERNMENT WARNING: (1) According to the Surgeon General, women should not drink alcoholic "
    26	    "beverages during pregnancy because of the risk of birth defects. (2) Consumption of alcoholic "
    27	    "beverages impairs your ability to drive a car or operate machinery, and may cause health problems."
    28	)
    29	ANCHOR = "GOVERNMENT WARNING"
    30	_ANCHOR_RE = re.compile(r"g\s*o\s*v\s*e\s*r\s*n\s*m\s*e\s*n\s*t\s*w\s*a\s*r\s*n\s*i\s*n\s*g", re.I)
    31	_CANON_FOLD = fold(CANONICAL)
    32	
    33	
    34	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    35	class WarningSpan:
    36	    text: str
    37	    lines: tuple[OcrLine, ...]
    38	    anchor_text: str  # the anchor as OCR read it, for the caps check
    39	
    40	
    41	def _anchor_score(text: str) -> int:
    42	    """Both words must be present (fuzzily); \"WARNING\" alone is not an anchor."""
    43	    t = fold(text)
    44	    return int(min(fuzz.partial_ratio("government", t), fuzz.partial_ratio("warning", t)))
    45	
    46	
    47	def _x_overlap(a: OcrLine, b: OcrLine) -> float:
    48	    """Horizontal overlap as a fraction of the narrower line."""
    49	    a0, a1 = min(p[0] for p in a.box), max(p[0] for p in a.box)
    50	    b0, b1 = min(p[0] for p in b.box), max(p[0] for p in b.box)
    51	    inter = max(0.0, min(a1, b1) - max(a0, b0))
    52	    return inter / max(1.0, min(a1 - a0, b1 - b0))
    53	
    54	
    55	def find_warning(lines: list[OcrLine]) -> WarningSpan | None:
    56	    """Locate the warning statement: an anchor line, then following lines while similarity to the
    57	    canonical text keeps improving. Returns the best span over all images."""
    58	    best: WarningSpan | None = None
    59	    best_sim = -1.0
    60	    for group in reading_order(lines).values():
    61	        for i, ln in enumerate(group):
    62	            if _anchor_score(ln.text) < 80:
    63	                continue
    64	            m = _ANCHOR_RE.search(ln.text)
    65	            anchor_text = m.group(0) if m else ln.text
    66	            acc: list[OcrLine] = []
    67	            local_best: tuple[float, list[OcrLine]] | None = None
    68	            declines = 0
    69	            for nxt in group[i:]:
    70	                if nxt is not ln and _x_overlap(ln, nxt) < 0.3:
    71	                    continue  # a different column or an unrelated block
    72	                acc.append(nxt)
    73	                sim = fuzz.ratio(_CANON_FOLD, fold(join_hyphenated([x.text for x in acc]))) / 100
    74	                if local_best is None or sim > local_best[0]:
    75	                    local_best = (sim, list(acc))
    76	                    declines = 0
    77	                else:
    78	                    declines += 1
    79	                    if declines >= 2:
    80	                        break
    81	                if "health problems" in fold(nxt.text) and len(acc) > 1:
    82	                    break
    83	            if local_best and local_best[0] > best_sim:
    84	                best_sim = local_best[0]
    85	                best = WarningSpan(
    86	                    text=join_hyphenated([x.text for x in local_best[1]]),
    87	                    lines=tuple(local_best[1]),
    88	                    anchor_text=anchor_text,
    89	                )
    90	    return best
    91	
    92	
    93	def _canon_form(s: str) -> str:
    94	    # Whitespace and typographic quotes are rendering, not wording; everything else must be literal.
    95	    return collapse_ws(unify_punctuation(s))
    96	
    97	
    98	def word_diff(expected: str, found: str) -> str | None:
    99	    """Compact word-level diff, e.g. '-may +can' or '-(2) '. None when identical."""
   100	    a, b = _canon_form(expected).split(" "), _canon_form(found).split(" ")
   101	    out: list[str] = []
   102	    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in difflib.SequenceMatcher(a=a, b=b, autojunk=False).get_opcodes():
   103	        if tag == "equal":
   104	            continue
   105	        if i2 > i1:
   106	            out.append("-" + " ".join(a[i1:i2]))
   107	        if j2 > j1:
   108	            out.append("+" + " ".join(b[j1:j2]))
   109	    return " | ".join(out) if out else None
   110	
   111	
   112	def compare_warning(found: str) -> tuple[bool, bool, float]:
   113	    """Returns (exact, case_only_difference, similarity 0-1)."""
   114	    canon, got = _canon_form(CANONICAL), _canon_form(found)
   115	    exact = canon == got
   116	    case_only = (not exact) and canon.casefold() == got.casefold()
   117	    similarity = fuzz.ratio(canon.casefold(), got.casefold()) / 100
   118	    return exact, case_only, similarity
   119	
   120	
   121	_PUNCT_ONLY = re.compile(r"^[^\w]+$")
   122	_STRIP = re.compile(r"[^\w]")
   123	# OCR confusables folded to one canonical letter: 0/o, 1/l/i/|/!, 5/s, 8/b, 2/z, 6/g
   124	_CONFUSABLE = str.maketrans({"0": "o", "1": "l", "i": "l", "|": "l", "!": "l", "5": "s", "8": "b", "2": "z", "6": "g"})
   125	
   126	
   127	def _canon_word(w: str) -> str:
   128	    decomposed = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", w)
   129	    letters = "".join(ch for ch in decomposed if not unicodedata.combining(ch))
   130	    return _STRIP.sub("", letters).casefold().translate(_CONFUSABLE)
   131	
   132	
   133	def _same_word_modulo_noise(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
   134	    """True when two tokens differ only by punctuation, case, accents, OCR confusables, or a single
   135	    character in a word of five or more letters (a real wording change replaces whole words)."""
   136	    ka, kb = _canon_word(a), _canon_word(b)
   137	    if ka == kb:
   138	        return True
   139	    return len(ka) >= 5 and len(kb) >= 5 and Levenshtein.distance(ka, kb) <= 1
   140	
   141	
   142	def classify_difference(expected: str, found: str) -> str:
   143	    """'exact' | 'noise' (punctuation, case, confusables) | 'wording' (a word added, dropped or replaced)."""
   144	    a = _canon_form(expected).split(" ")
   145	    b = _canon_form(found).split(" ")
   146	    if a == b:
   147	        return "exact"
   148	    verdict = "noise"
   149	    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in difflib.SequenceMatcher(
   150	        a=[w.casefold() for w in a], b=[w.casefold() for w in b], autojunk=False
   151	    ).get_opcodes():
   152	        if tag == "equal":
   153	            continue
   154	        left, right = a[i1:i2], b[j1:j2]
   155	        if tag == "replace" and len(left) == len(right):
   156	            if all(_same_word_modulo_noise(x, y) for x, y in zip(left, right, strict=True)):
   157	                continue
   158	            return "wording"
   159	        if tag == "replace":
   160	            # e.g. "Surgeon General," read as "SurgeonGeneral," (merged) or split words
   161	            if _canon_word("".join(left)) == _canon_word("".join(right)):
   162	                continue
   163	            return "wording"
   164	        # pure insert or delete: punctuation-only tokens are noise; anything with a letter or digit
   165	        # (a word, or a clause number like "(2)") is a wording change
   166	        tokens = left or right
   167	        if all(_PUNCT_ONLY.match(t) for t in tokens):
   168	            continue
   169	        return "wording"
   170	    return verdict
   171	
   172	
   173	def anchor_caps_status(anchor_text: str) -> tuple[Status, str]:
   174	    letters = [c for c in anchor_text if c.isalpha()]
   175	    if letters and all(c.isupper() for c in letters):
   176	        return Status.match, "GOVERNMENT WARNING appears in capital letters."
   177	    return (
   178	        Status.needs_review,
   179	        f"Read as '{collapse_ws(anchor_text)}', which is not all capitals. "
   180	        "Capital letters are required (27 CFR 16.22(a)(2)). Confirm on the image.",
   181	    )
   182	
   183	
   184	def build_report(lines: list[OcrLine], *, mismatch_similarity: float) -> WarningReport:
   185	    """Assess the warning statement. ``assessment`` drives the verdict:
   186	    exact -> pass; case/noise -> Needs review; wording/absent -> issue."""
   187	    span = find_warning(lines)
   188	    if span is None:
   189	        return WarningReport(
   190	            present=False,
   191	            exact=False,
   192	            assessment="absent",
   193	            similarity=0.0,
   194	            found_text=None,
   195	            diff=None,
   196	            anchor_caps=Status.not_found,
   197	            anchor_bold=Status.not_checked,
   198	            body_not_bold=Status.not_checked,
   199	            notes=[
   200	                "No GOVERNMENT WARNING statement was found on any image. It is mandatory on all alcoholic "
   201	                "beverages of 0.5% alcohol or more (27 CFR 16.21). If the warning is on a label image not "
   202	                "uploaded, add that image."
   203	            ],
   204	        )
   205	    exact, case_only, similarity = compare_warning(span.text)
   206	    caps_status, caps_note = anchor_caps_status(span.anchor_text)
   207	    if exact:
   208	        assessment = "exact"
   209	    elif case_only:
   210	        assessment = "case"
   211	    else:
   212	        assessment = classify_difference(CANONICAL, span.text)
   213	        if assessment == "noise" and similarity < mismatch_similarity:
   214	            assessment = "wording"  # too many differences to call it noise
   215	    notes = {
   216	        "exact": "Wording is exact (27 CFR 16.21).",
   217	        "case": "Wording matches except for letter case. Confirm the statement's capitalization on the image.",
   218	        "noise": (
   219	            "Wording matches apart from punctuation, an accent, or single characters as read, which is usually "
   220	            "OCR noise (a dropped colon, a '1' read as 'i', a stray accent). Compare the diff with the image."
   221	        ),
   222	        "wording": (
   223	            "Wording differs from the required text: a word is missing, added or changed. "
   224	            "The statement must be word for word (27 CFR 16.21)."
   225	        ),
   226	    }
   227	    return WarningReport(
   228	        present=True,
   229	        exact=exact,
   230	        assessment=assessment,
   231	        similarity=round(similarity, 4),
   232	        found_text=span.text,
   233	        diff=None if exact else word_diff(CANONICAL, span.text),
   234	        anchor_caps=caps_status,
   235	        anchor_bold=Status.not_checked,
   236	        body_not_bold=Status.not_checked,
   237	        evidence=[Evidence(image_index=ln.image_index, box=ln.box, text=ln.text) for ln in span.lines],
   238	        notes=[
   239	            notes[assessment],
   240	            caps_note,
   241	            "Bold type (anchor bold, remainder not bold) is not assessed automatically in this build; "
   242	            "check it on the image (27 CFR 16.22(a)(2)).",
   243	        ],
   244	    )

     1	from __future__ import annotations
     2	
     3	import json
     4	from typing import cast
     5	
     6	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, Form, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile
     7	from pydantic import ValidationError
     8	
     9	from app import __version__
    10	from app.config import Settings
    11	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool
    12	from app.pipeline.images import ImageError
    13	from app.schemas import (
    14	    ApplicationFields,
    15	    CompareRequest,
    16	    CompareResponse,
    17	    CompareResponseItem,
    18	    ExtractResponse,
    19	    HealthResponse,
    20	    VerifyResponse,
    21	)
    22	from app.security import request_id_of
    23	from app.services import Upload, engine_info, extract, verify
    24	
    25	router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1")
    26	
    27	
    28	def _settings(request: Request) -> Settings:
    29	    return cast(Settings, request.app.state.settings)
    30	
    31	
    32	def _pool(request: Request) -> OcrPool:
    33	    pool = cast(OcrPool, request.app.state.pool)
    34	    if not pool.ready:
    35	        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="not_ready", headers={"Retry-After": "3"})
    36	    return pool
    37	
    38	
    39	async def _read_uploads(files: list[UploadFile], settings: Settings) -> list[Upload]:
    40	    if not files:
    41	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="At least one image is required.")
    42	    if len(files) > settings.max_images_per_application:
    43	        raise HTTPException(
    44	            status_code=422, detail=f"At most {settings.max_images_per_application} images per application."
    45	        )
    46	    uploads: list[Upload] = []
    47	    for f in files:
    48	        chunks: list[bytes] = []
    49	        total = 0
    50	        while True:
    51	            chunk = await f.read(1024 * 1024)
    52	            if not chunk:
    53	                break
    54	            total += len(chunk)
    55	            if total > settings.max_image_bytes:
    56	                mb = settings.max_image_bytes // (1024 * 1024)
    57	                raise ImageError(
    58	                    "image_too_large",
    59	                    f"'{f.filename}' is larger than the {mb} MB per-image limit.",
    60	                    "Export the artwork at a smaller size or higher compression.",
    61	                )
    62	            chunks.append(chunk)
    63	        if total == 0:
    64	            raise ImageError("empty_file", f"'{f.filename}' is empty.", "Choose the image file again.")
    65	        uploads.append(Upload(data=b"".join(chunks), filename=f.filename))
    66	    return uploads
    67	
    68	
    69	def _parse_application(raw: str) -> ApplicationFields:
    70	    try:
    71	        return ApplicationFields.model_validate(json.loads(raw))
    72	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
    73	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="application must be a JSON object.") from exc
    74	    except ValidationError as exc:
    75	        first = exc.errors()[0]
    76	        loc = ".".join(str(p) for p in first["loc"])
    77	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"application.{loc}: {first['msg']}") from exc
    78	
    79	
    80	@router.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=["system"])
    81	async def health(request: Request) -> HealthResponse:
    82	    pool = request.app.state.pool
    83	    s = _settings(request)
    84	    return HealthResponse(
    85	        status="ok" if pool.ready else ("failed" if pool.error else "starting"),
    86	        ready=pool.ready,
    87	        error=pool.error,
    88	        engine=engine_info(pool),
    89	        max_concurrency=pool.workers,
    90	        in_flight=pool.in_flight,
    91	        requests_in_flight=request.app.state.limiter.in_flight,
    92	        version=__version__,
    93	        git_sha=s.git_sha,
    94	    )
    95	
    96	
    97	@router.get("/ready", tags=["system"], summary="Readiness probe: 200 once the OCR engines are warm, else 503")
    98	async def ready(request: Request) -> dict[str, bool]:
    99	    pool = request.app.state.pool
   100	    if not pool.ready:
   101	        detail = f"warm-up failed: {pool.error}" if pool.error else "not_ready"
   102	        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=detail, headers={"Retry-After": "3"})
   103	    return {"ready": True}
   104	
   105	
   106	@router.post(
   107	    "/verify",
   108	    response_model=VerifyResponse,
   109	    tags=["verification"],
   110	    summary="Verify one application against one or more label images",
   111	)
   112	async def verify_route(
   113	    request: Request,
   114	    application: str = Form(..., description="JSON object with the application fields"),
   115	    images: list[UploadFile] = File(..., description="1 to 6 label images (front, back, neck...)"),
   116	    settings: Settings = Depends(_settings),
   117	) -> VerifyResponse:
   118	    pool = _pool(request)
   119	    app_fields = _parse_application(application)
   120	    uploads = await _read_uploads(images, settings)
   121	    interactive = request.headers.get("x-batch", "0") != "1"
   122	    return await verify(app_fields, uploads, settings, pool, interactive=interactive, request_id=request_id_of(request))
   123	
   124	
   125	@router.post(
   126	    "/extract",
   127	    response_model=ExtractResponse,
   128	    tags=["verification"],
   129	    summary="Read a label without application data (batch and extract-only mode)",
   130	)
   131	async def extract_route(
   132	    request: Request,
   133	    images: list[UploadFile] = File(...),
   134	    settings: Settings = Depends(_settings),
   135	) -> ExtractResponse:
   136	    pool = _pool(request)
   137	    uploads = await _read_uploads(images, settings)
   138	    interactive = request.headers.get("x-batch", "0") != "1"
   139	    return await extract(uploads, settings, pool, interactive=interactive, request_id=request_id_of(request))
   140	
   141	
   142	@router.post(
   143	    "/compare",
   144	    response_model=CompareResponse,
   145	    tags=["verification"],
   146	    summary="Compare application data with previously extracted lines (no OCR, fast)",
   147	)
   148	async def compare_route(
   149	    body: CompareRequest, request: Request, settings: Settings = Depends(_settings)
   150	) -> CompareResponse:
   151	    from app.pipeline.compare import compare
   152	
   153	    if len(body.items) > settings.max_compare_items:
   154	        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"At most {settings.max_compare_items} items per compare call.")
   155	    results = [
   156	        CompareResponseItem(
   157	            item_id=item.item_id, **compare(item.application, item.lines, item.images, settings).model_dump()
   158	        )
   159	        for item in body.items
   160	    ]
   161	    return CompareResponse(request_id=request_id_of(request), results=results)

     1	from __future__ import annotations

    10	from app.csvio import parse_csv, template_csv

    14	router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/csv", tags=["batch"])

    17	class CsvRowOut(BaseModel):
    18	    row_number: int
    19	    application: ApplicationFields | None
    20	    images: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    21	    errors: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    22

    24	class CsvParseResponse(BaseModel):
    25	    request_id: str
    26	    rows: list[CsvRowOut]

    29	    delimiter: str
    30	    warnings: list[str]

    33	@router.get("/template", response_class=PlainTextResponse, summary="Download the batch CSV template")
    34	async def csv_template() -> PlainTextResponse:
    35	    return PlainTextResponse(
    36	        template_csv(),
    37	        media_type="text/csv",
    38	        headers={"Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="label-check-batch-template.csv"'},
    39	    )

    42	@router.post("/parse", response_model=CsvParseResponse, summary="Parse and validate a batch CSV of application rows")
    43	async def csv_parse(request: Request, file: UploadFile = File(...)) -> CsvParseResponse:
    44	    settings = cast(Settings, request.app.state.settings)
    45	    data = await file.read(settings.max_csv_bytes + 1)
    46	    if len(data) > settings.max_csv_bytes:
    47	        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail=f"CSV larger than {settings.max_csv_bytes // 1024} KB.")
    48	    result = parse_csv(data, max_rows=settings.max_csv_rows)
    49	    return CsvParseResponse(
    50	        request_id=request_id_of(request),
    51	        rows=[
    52	            CsvRowOut(row_number=r.row_number, application=r.application, images=r.images, errors=r.errors)
    53	            for r in result.rows
    54	        ],
    55	        columns=result.columns,
    56	        unmapped_columns=result.unmapped_columns,
    57	        delimiter=result.delimiter,
    58	        warnings=result.warnings,
    59	    )

     1	"""Pydantic models shared by the API and the pipeline.
     2	
     3	Coordinates: every box is a quadrilateral of four (x, y) points in the *oriented original
     4	image* space (after EXIF transpose, before any downscaling). The client draws in that space.
     5	"""
