===== FILE: app/security.py =====
     1	"""Security headers, request ids, body-size guard, admission caps before body parsing, error envelope."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import logging
     6	import threading
     7	import time
     8	import uuid
     9	from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable, Mapping
    10	
    11	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request, Response
    12	from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
    13	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    14	from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
    15	from starlette.types import ASGIApp
    16	
    17	from app.config import Settings
    18	from app.ocr.pool import BusyError
    19	from app.pipeline.images import ImageError
    20	from app.schemas import ErrorResponse
    21	
    22	log = logging.getLogger("app.request")
    23	
    24	CSP = (
    25	    "default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' blob: data:; style-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; "
    26	    "connect-src 'self'; font-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'"
    27	)
    28	SECURITY_HEADERS = {
    29	    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    30	    "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
    31	    "Permissions-Policy": "camera=(self), microphone=(), geolocation=()",
    32	    "Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy": "same-origin",
    33	    "Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy": "same-origin",
    34	    "Content-Security-Policy": CSP,
    35	}
    36	# Endpoints that parse bodies and cost CPU; capped before the body is read.
    37	METERED_PREFIXES = ("/api/v1/verify", "/api/v1/extract", "/api/v1/compare", "/api/v1/csv/parse")
    38	
    39	
    40	def request_id_of(request: Request) -> str:
    41	    return getattr(request.state, "request_id", "") or ""
    42	
    43	
    44	def apply_security_headers(response: Response) -> Response:
    45	    for k, v in SECURITY_HEADERS.items():
    46	        response.headers[k] = v
    47	    response.headers.setdefault("Cache-Control", "no-store")
    48	    return response
    49	
    50	
    51	def error_response(
    52	    status: int,
    53	    code: str,
    54	    message: str,
    55	    hint: str | None = None,
    56	    request_id: str | None = None,
    57	    headers: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    58	) -> JSONResponse:
    59	    body = ErrorResponse(code=code, message=message, hint=hint, request_id=request_id).model_dump()
    60	    resp = JSONResponse(status_code=status, content=body, headers=dict(headers or {}))
    61	    if request_id:
    62	        resp.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
    63	    apply_security_headers(resp)
    64	    return resp
    65	
    66	
    67	class AdmissionLimiter:
    68	    """Per-client and global caps on concurrent metered requests, enforced before body parsing.
    69	
    70	    Client identity: the connection address, or, when ``trust_proxy`` is set, the LAST value of
    71	    X-Forwarded-For, which is the one appended by the trusted ingress (the first value can be
    72	    forged by the client).
    73	    """
    74	
    75	    def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
    76	        self.per_client = settings.per_client_inflight
    77	        self.global_limit = settings.global_inflight
    78	        self.trust_proxy = settings.trust_proxy
    79	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
    80	        self._counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    81	        self._total = 0
    82	
    83	    def client_id(self, request: Request) -> str:
    84	        if self.trust_proxy:
    85	            fwd = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for")
    86	            if fwd:
    87	                return fwd.split(",")[-1].strip()
    88	        return request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
    89	
    90	    def acquire(self, request: Request) -> str | None:
    91	        """Returns None when admitted, else the reason code."""
    92	        cid = self.client_id(request)
    93	        with self._lock:
    94	            if self._total >= self.global_limit:
    95	                return "service_busy"
    96	            if self._counts.get(cid, 0) >= self.per_client:
    97	                return "too_many_inflight"
    98	            self._counts[cid] = self._counts.get(cid, 0) + 1
    99	            self._total += 1
   100	        request.state.admitted_client = cid
   101	        return None
   102	
   103	    def release(self, request: Request) -> None:
   104	        cid = getattr(request.state, "admitted_client", None)
   105	        if cid is None:
   106	            return
   107	        with self._lock:
   108	            self._counts[cid] -= 1
   109	            if self._counts[cid] <= 0:
   110	                del self._counts[cid]
   111	            self._total -= 1
   112	
   113	    @property
   114	    def in_flight(self) -> int:
   115	        return self._total
   116	
   117	
   118	class SecurityMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
   119	    """Request id, Content-Length guard, admission caps, security headers, Server-Timing.
   120	    Logs method, path, status and milliseconds only."""
   121	
   122	    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings, limiter: AdmissionLimiter) -> None:
   123	        super().__init__(app)
   124	        self.settings = settings
   125	        self.limiter = limiter
   126	
   127	    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: Callable[[Request], Awaitable[Response]]) -> Response:
   128	        rid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
   129	        request.state.request_id = rid
   130	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
   131	        metered = request.method == "POST" and request.url.path.startswith(METERED_PREFIXES)
   132	        if request.method in ("POST", "PUT", "PATCH"):
   133	            length = request.headers.get("content-length")
   134	            if length is None:
   135	                return error_response(411, "length_required", "Uploads must declare Content-Length.", request_id=rid)
   136	            try:
   137	                declared = int(length)
   138	                if declared < 0:
   139	                    raise ValueError
   140	            except ValueError:
   141	                return error_response(400, "bad_request", "Content-Length is not a valid number.", request_id=rid)
   142	            if declared > self.settings.max_request_bytes:
   143	                mb = self.settings.max_request_bytes // (1024 * 1024)
   144	                return error_response(
   145	                    413,
   146	                    "request_too_large",
   147	                    f"The upload is larger than the {mb} MB request limit.",
   148	                    "Send fewer or smaller images per request.",
   149	                    request_id=rid,
   150	                )
   151	        admitted = False
   152	        if metered:
   153	            reason = self.limiter.acquire(request)
   154	            if reason == "too_many_inflight":
   155	                return error_response(
   156	                    429,
   157	                    reason,
   158	                    "Too many requests in flight from this client.",
   159	                    "Limit concurrent uploads and retry shortly.",
   160	                    request_id=rid,
   161	                    headers={"Retry-After": "1"},
   162	                )
   163	            if reason == "service_busy":
   164	                return error_response(
   165	                    503,
   166	                    reason,
   167	                    "The service is handling as many requests as it can right now.",
   168	                    "Retry in a moment.",
   169	                    request_id=rid,
   170	                    headers={"Retry-After": "2"},
   171	                )
   172	            admitted = True
   173	        try:
   174	            response = await call_next(request)
   175	        finally:
   176	            if admitted:
   177	                self.limiter.release(request)
   178	        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = rid
   179	        response.headers["Server-Timing"] = f"total;dur={(time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000:.0f}"
   180	        apply_security_headers(response)
   181	        if request.url.path.startswith("/api/"):
   182	            log.info(
   183	                "%s %s %s %.0fms rid=%s",
   184	                request.method,
   185	                request.url.path,
   186	                response.status_code,
   187	                (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000,
   188	                rid,
   189	            )
   190	        return response
   191	
   192	
   193	def install_error_handlers(app: FastAPI) -> None:
   194	    @app.exception_handler(ImageError)
   195	    async def _image_error(request: Request, exc: ImageError) -> JSONResponse:
   196	        status = {"unsupported_format": 415, "image_too_large": 413}.get(exc.code, 400)
   197	        return error_response(status, exc.code, exc.message, exc.hint, request_id_of(request))
   198	
   199	    @app.exception_handler(BusyError)
   200	    async def _busy(request: Request, exc: BusyError) -> JSONResponse:
   201	        return error_response(
   202	            429,
   203	            "busy",
   204	            "The verification service is at capacity right now.",
   205	            "Retry in a moment; batch clients back off automatically.",
   206	            request_id_of(request),
   207	            headers={"Retry-After": str(exc.retry_after)},
   208	        )
   209	
   210	    @app.exception_handler(HTTPException)
   211	    async def _http(request: Request, exc: HTTPException) -> JSONResponse:
   212	        detail = exc.detail if isinstance(exc.detail, str) else "error"
   213	        messages = {
   214	            "not_ready": ("The OCR engine is still starting.", "Retry in a few seconds."),
   215	        }
   216	        hint: str | None
   217	        if detail.startswith("warm-up failed"):
   218	            message, hint = detail, "The service cannot start; see the container logs."
   219	        else:
   220	            message, hint = messages.get(detail, (detail, None))
   221	        return error_response(exc.status_code, detail, message, hint, request_id_of(request), headers=exc.headers)
   222	
   223	    @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
   224	    async def _validation(request: Request, exc: RequestValidationError) -> JSONResponse:
   225	        first = exc.errors()[0] if exc.errors() else {}
   226	        loc = ".".join(str(p) for p in first.get("loc", []) if p not in ("body",))
   227	        return error_response(
   228	            422,
   229	            "invalid_request",
   230	            f"{loc or 'request'}: {first.get('msg', 'invalid')}",
   231	            "Check the field names and values.",
   232	            request_id_of(request),
   233	        )
   234	
   235	    @app.exception_handler(Exception)
   236	    async def _unhandled(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
   237	        log.exception("unhandled error rid=%s", request_id_of(request))
   238	        return error_response(
   239	            500,
   240	            "internal_error",
   241	            "Something went wrong on our side.",
   242	            "Try again; if it persists, report the request id.",
   243	            request_id_of(request),
   244	        )

===== FILE: app/services.py =====
     1	"""Orchestration: bytes in, verified result out. The only module that touches both the OCR pool
     2	and the pure pipeline."""
     3	
     4	from __future__ import annotations
     5	
     6	import asyncio
     7	import time
     8	import uuid
     9	from dataclasses import dataclass
    10	
    11	import numpy as np
    12	
    13	from app.config import Settings
    14	from app.ocr.base import RawLine
    15	from app.ocr.pool import OcrPool, Runner
    16	from app.pipeline.compare import compare
    17	from app.pipeline.extract import extract_fields
    18	from app.pipeline.images import DecodedImage, decode_image, rotate_array, to_canonical
    19	from app.schemas import (
    20	    ApplicationFields,
    21	    EngineInfo,
    22	    ExtractResponse,
    23	    ImageInfo,
    24	    ImageQuality,
    25	    OcrLine,
    26	    Timing,
    27	    VerifyResponse,
    28	)
    29	
    30	
    31	def new_request_id() -> str:
    32	    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    33	
    34	
    35	@dataclass
    36	class Upload:
    37	    data: bytes
    38	    filename: str | None
    39	
    40	
    41	@dataclass
    42	class ProcessedImage:
    43	    info: ImageInfo
    44	    lines: list[OcrLine]
    45	    queue_ms: int
    46	    ocr_ms: int
    47	
    48	
    49	def _to_lines(raw: list[RawLine], dec: DecodedImage, index: int, degrees: int, rot_w: int, rot_h: int) -> list[OcrLine]:
    50	    return [
    51	        OcrLine(
    52	            image_index=index,
    53	            text=r.text,
    54	            confidence=round(r.confidence, 4),
    55	            box=to_canonical(r.box, scale=dec.scale, degrees=degrees, rot_w=rot_w, rot_h=rot_h),
    56	        )
    57	        for r in raw
    58	    ]
    59	
    60	
    61	def _read_score(raw: list[RawLine]) -> float:
    62	    """How much confident text was read: sum of confidences of lines at or above 0.5."""
    63	    return float(sum(r.confidence for r in raw if r.confidence >= 0.5))
    64	
    65	
    66	def _quality(raw: list[RawLine]) -> ImageQuality:
    67	    if not raw:
    68	        return ImageQuality(
    69	            mean_confidence=0.0,
    70	            line_count=0,
    71	            readable=False,
    72	            reason="No text was detected. The image may be blank, too small, or not a label.",
    73	        )
    74	    mean = float(np.mean([r.confidence for r in raw]))
    75	    if mean < 0.6:
    76	        return ImageQuality(
    77	            mean_confidence=round(mean, 3),
    78	            line_count=len(raw),
    79	            readable=False,
    80	            reason="Text was detected but read with low confidence. The image may be blurry, "
    81	            "low contrast, or photographed at an angle. Request a clearer image.",
    82	        )
    83	    return ImageQuality(mean_confidence=round(mean, 3), line_count=len(raw), readable=True)
    84	
    85	
    86	async def process_image(
    87	    up: Upload, index: int, settings: Settings, run: Runner, *, queue_ms: int = 0
    88	) -> ProcessedImage:
    89	    """Decode one upload and read it on the given slot, retrying sideways orientations when the
    90	    first read is poor (the one failure mode the engine does not recover on its own)."""
    91	    dec = decode_image(
    92	        up.data, max_pixels=settings.max_image_pixels, max_side=settings.ocr_max_side, filename=up.filename
    93	    )
    94	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    95	    raw = await run(dec.array)
    96	    h, w = dec.array.shape[:2]
    97	    best = (_read_score(raw), raw, 0, w, h)
    98	    mean = float(np.mean([r.confidence for r in raw])) if raw else 0.0
    99	    if mean < settings.ocr_low_conf_retry or len(raw) < settings.ocr_min_lines_retry:
   100	        for degrees in (90, 270):
   101	            rot = rotate_array(dec.array, degrees)
   102	            raw2 = await run(rot)
   103	            if _read_score(raw2) > best[0] * 1.15:
   104	                best = (_read_score(raw2), raw2, degrees, rot.shape[1], rot.shape[0])
   105	    ocr_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
   106	    _, raw_best, degrees, rw, rh = best
   107	    lines = _to_lines(raw_best, dec, index, degrees, rw, rh)
   108	    info = ImageInfo(
   109	        index=index,
   110	        filename=up.filename,
   111	        width=dec.width,
   112	        height=dec.height,
   113	        format=dec.format,
   114	        rotated_degrees=degrees,
   115	        quality=_quality(raw_best),
   116	    )
   117	    return ProcessedImage(info=info, lines=lines, queue_ms=queue_ms, ocr_ms=ocr_ms)
   118	
   119	
   120	async def process_images(
   121	    uploads: list[Upload], settings: Settings, pool: OcrPool, *, interactive: bool
   122	) -> list[ProcessedImage]:
   123	    """Interactive: each image takes its own slot so a front+back pair costs one image-time.
   124	    Batch: one slot is held for all the images of the request, so it can never be refused halfway."""
   125	    if interactive:
   126	
   127	        async def one(i: int, u: Upload) -> ProcessedImage:
   128	            async with pool.slot(interactive=True) as (run, queue_ms):
   129	                return await process_image(u, i, settings, run, queue_ms=queue_ms)
   130	
   131	        return list(await asyncio.gather(*(one(i, u) for i, u in enumerate(uploads))))
   132	    async with pool.slot(interactive=False) as (run, queue_ms):
   133	        return [await process_image(u, i, settings, run, queue_ms=queue_ms) for i, u in enumerate(uploads)]
   134	
   135	
   136	def engine_info(pool: OcrPool) -> EngineInfo:
   137	    return EngineInfo(name="rapidocr-onnxruntime", models=pool.info(), workers=pool.workers)
   138	
   139	
   140	def _timing(t0: float, processed: list[ProcessedImage]) -> Timing:
   141	    return Timing(
   142	        total_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
   143	        queue_ms=max((p.queue_ms for p in processed), default=0),
   144	        ocr_ms=[p.ocr_ms for p in processed],
   145	    )
   146	
   147	
   148	async def verify(
   149	    app: ApplicationFields,
   150	    uploads: list[Upload],
   151	    settings: Settings,
   152	    pool: OcrPool,
   153	    *,
   154	    interactive: bool = True,
   155	    request_id: str | None = None,
   156	) -> VerifyResponse:
   157	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   158	    processed = await process_images(uploads, settings, pool, interactive=interactive)
   159	    lines = [ln for p in processed for ln in p.lines]
   160	    images = [p.info for p in processed]
   161	    result = compare(app, lines, images, settings)
   162	    return VerifyResponse(
   163	        request_id=request_id or new_request_id(),
   164	        application=app,
   165	        images=images,
   166	        lines=lines,
   167	        timing=_timing(t0, processed),
   168	        engine=engine_info(pool),
   169	        **result.model_dump(),
   170	    )
   171	
   172	
   173	async def extract(
   174	    uploads: list[Upload],
   175	    settings: Settings,
   176	    pool: OcrPool,
   177	    *,
   178	    interactive: bool = False,
   179	    request_id: str | None = None,
   180	) -> ExtractResponse:
   181	    t0 = time.perf_counter()
   182	    processed = await process_images(uploads, settings, pool, interactive=interactive)
   183	    lines = [ln for p in processed for ln in p.lines]
   184	    return ExtractResponse(
   185	        request_id=request_id or new_request_id(),
   186	        images=[p.info for p in processed],
   187	        lines=lines,
   188	        fields=extract_fields(lines),
   189	        timing=_timing(t0, processed),
   190	        engine=engine_info(pool),
   191	    )

     1	// Controller for the single-application screen. Vanilla ES module, no build step.

    39	function showFormError(msg, hint = "") {

    44	function humanSize(n) { return n > 1024 * 1024 ? `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB` : `${Math.round(n / 1024)} KB`; }
    45	
    46	function renderFileList() {
    47	  const list = $("#file-list");
    48	  list.replaceChildren(...state.files.map((f, i) => {
    49	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
    50	    const img = el("img", { src: url, alt: "" });
    51	    img.addEventListener("load", () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), { once: true });
    52	    return el("li", {}, [
    53	      img,
    54	      el("div", {}, [el("div", { class: "file-name", text: f.name }), el("div", { class: "file-meta", text: `${f.type || "image"} · ${humanSize(f.size)}` })]),
    55	      el("button", { type: "button", class: "usa-button usa-button--unstyled", text: "Remove", "aria-label": `Remove ${f.name}`, onclick: () => { state.files.splice(i, 1); renderFileList(); } }),
    56	    ]);
    57	  }));
    58	  $("#clear-files").hidden = state.files.length === 0;
    59	}
    60	
    61	function addFiles(fileList) {
    62	  const problems = [];
    63	  for (const f of fileList) {
    64	    if (state.files.length >= MAX_IMAGES) { problems.push(`Only ${MAX_IMAGES} images per application; "${f.name}" was not added.`); break; }
    65	    if (f.type && !ACCEPT.test(f.type)) { problems.push(`"${f.name}" is not a supported image type (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, TIFF, BMP).`); continue; }
    66	    if (f.size > MAX_BYTES) { problems.push(`"${f.name}" is ${humanSize(f.size)}; the limit is 10 MB per image.`); continue; }
    67	    if (f.size === 0) { problems.push(`"${f.name}" is empty.`); continue; }
    68	    if (state.files.some((g) => g.name === f.name && g.size === f.size)) continue; // same file twice
    69	    state.files.push(f);
    70	  }
    71	  renderFileList();
    72	  showFormError(problems.join(" "));
    73	}
    74	
    75	function wireDropzone() {
    76	  const dz = $("#dropzone");
    77	  const input = $("#file-input");
    78	  input.addEventListener("change", () => { addFiles(input.files); input.value = ""; });
    79	  ["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add("is-dragover"); }));
    80	  ["dragleave", "drop"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.remove("is-dragover"); }));
    81	  dz.addEventListener("drop", (e) => addFiles(e.dataTransfer.files));
    82	  $("#clear-files").addEventListener("click", () => { state.files = []; renderFileList(); });
    83	  document.addEventListener("paste", (e) => {
    84	    const imgs = [...(e.clipboardData?.files || [])].filter((f) => ACCEPT.test(f.type));
    85	    if (imgs.length) addFiles(imgs);
    86	  });
    87	}

    90	function readApplication() {
    91	  const form = $("#check-form");
    92	  const val = (id) => form.querySelector(`#${id}`).value.trim();
    93	  const app = {
    94	    beverage_type: form.querySelector("input[name=beverage_type]:checked").value,
    95	    brand_name: val("brand_name"),
    96	    class_type: val("class_type"),
    97	    alcohol_content: val("alcohol_content") || null,
    98	    net_contents: val("net_contents"),
    99	    bottler: val("bottler") || null,
   100	    country_of_origin: val("country_of_origin") || null,
   101	    imported: form.querySelector("#imported").checked,
   102	    application_id: val("application_id") || null,
   103	  };
   104	  return app;
   105	}

   107	function fillApplication(app) {
   108	  const form = $("#check-form");
   109	  form.querySelector(`input[name=beverage_type][value=${app.beverage_type}]`).checked = true;
   110	  for (const id of ["brand_name", "class_type", "alcohol_content", "net_contents", "bottler", "country_of_origin", "application_id"]) {
   111	    form.querySelector(`#${id}`).value = app[id] || "";
   112	  }
   113	  form.querySelector("#imported").checked = Boolean(app.imported);
   114	  const hasOptional = app.country_of_origin || app.application_id || app.imported;
   115	  const btn = form.querySelector("[aria-controls=optional-fields]");
   116	  if (hasOptional && btn.getAttribute("aria-expanded") !== "true") btn.click();
   117	}

   119	function validate(app) {
   120	  const missing = [];
   121	  if (!state.files.length) missing.push("at least one label image");
   122	  if (!app.brand_name) missing.push("the brand name");
   123	  if (!app.class_type) missing.push("the class / type");
   124	  if (!app.net_contents) missing.push("the net contents");
   125	  return missing;
   126	}

   129	async function loadSamples() {
   130	  try { state.samples = await (await fetch("/static/samples/samples.json")).json(); } catch { state.samples = []; }
   131	}
   132	async function useSample(id) {
   133	  const s = (state.samples || []).find((x) => x.id === id);
   134	  if (!s) return;
   135	  $("#sample-blurb").textContent = s.blurb;
   136	  fillApplication(s.application);
   137	  setStatus("Loading sample images…", true);
   138	  const files = [];
   139	  for (const name of s.images) {
   140	    const blob = await (await fetch(`/static/samples/${name}`)).blob();
   141	    files.push(new File([blob], name, { type: blob.type || "image/png" }));
   142	  }
   143	  state.files = files;
   144	  renderFileList();
   145	  setStatus("");
   146	  await runCheck();
   147	}

   149	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ check
   150	function selectCheck(check) {
   151	  state.activeId = check.id;
   152	  drawOverlays(state.overlays, state.result.checks, state.result.warning, check.id);
   153	  document.querySelectorAll(".checklist tbody tr").forEach((tr) => tr.classList.toggle("is-active", tr.dataset.check === check.id));
   154	  const ev = check.evidence && check.evidence[0];
   155	  if (ev) {
   156	    const fig = $("#figures").children[ev.image_index];
   157	    fig?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches ? "auto" : "smooth", block: "nearest" });
   158	  }
   159	}
   160	
   161	async function runCheck() {
   162	  const app = readApplication();
   163	  const missing = validate(app);
   164	  if (missing.length) { showFormError(`Please add ${missing.join(", ")}.`); return; }
   165	  showFormError("");
   166	  const btn = $("#check-btn");
   167	  btn.disabled = true;
   168	  setStatus(`Reading ${state.files.length} image${state.files.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}…`, true);
   169	  const t0 = performance.now();
   170	  try {
   171	    const result = await verify(state.files, app);
   172	    state.result = result;
   173	    state.activeId = null;
   174	    const crops = await makeCrops(state.files, result.images, result.checks);
   175	    renderVerdict($("#verdict"), result);
   176	    renderChecklist($("#checklist"), result.checks, selectCheck, crops);
   177	    renderWarning($("#warning-report"), result.warning, selectCheck);
   178	    state.overlays = renderFigures($("#figures"), result.images, state.files, result.lines);
   179	    drawOverlays(state.overlays, result.checks, result.warning, null);
   180	    renderOcrLines($("#ocr-lines"), result.lines);
   181	    const results = $("#results");
   182	    results.hidden = false;
   183	    results.focus({ preventScroll: false });
   184	    results.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
   185	    setStatus(`Done in ${((performance.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)} s.`);
   186	  } catch (err) {
   187	    if (err instanceof ApiError) {
   188	      const extra = err.status === 429 ? " The service is busy; try again in a moment." : "";
   189	      showFormError(err.message + extra, err.hint);
   190	    } else {
   191	      showFormError("Could not reach the verification service.", "Check your connection and try again.");
   192	    }
   193	    setStatus("");
   194	  } finally {
   195	    btn.disabled = false;
   196	  }
   197	}
   198	
   199	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ boot
   200	async function boot() {
   201	  await loadSprite();
   202	  wireDropzone();
   203	  routeFromHash();
   204	  window.addEventListener("hashchange", routeFromHash);
   205	  $("#check-form").addEventListener("submit", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); runCheck(); });
   206	  document.querySelectorAll("[data-sample]").forEach((b) => b.addEventListener("click", () => useSample(b.dataset.sample)));
   207	  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", (e) => { if (state.result) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ""; } });
   208	  loadSamples();
   209	  try {
   210	    const h = await health();
   211	    $("#build-info").textContent = `Build ${h.git_sha} · engine ${h.engine.name} · ${h.max_concurrency} worker${h.max_concurrency === 1 ? "" : "s"}.`;
   212	    if (!h.ready) setStatus("The text-recognition engine is starting up…", true);
   213	  } catch { /* footer stays generic */ }
   214	}
   215	
   216	boot();

===== FILE: app/static/batch.js =====
     1	// Batch screen. The browser orchestrates: every image is read once (/extract), images are paired
     2	// to application rows explicitly (CSV "images" column or filename prefix), each application is
     3	// compared (/compare), rows stream into the table, the agent records decisions, and everything can
     4	// be exported. The server stays stateless; a refresh clears the session and the page says so.
     5	import { ApiError, compare, extract, health } from "./api.js";
     6	import { drawOverlays, el, makeCrops, renderChecklist, renderFigures, renderWarning, statusTag } from "./render.js";
     7	
     8	const VERDICT_WORD = { ready_for_approval: "Ready", needs_review: "Needs review", issues_found: "Issues found", unreadable: "Unreadable" };
     9	const VERDICT_STATUS = { ready_for_approval: "match", needs_review: "needs_review", issues_found: "mismatch", unreadable: "not_found" };
    10	const SEVERITY = { issues_found: 0, unreadable: 1, needs_review: 2, ready_for_approval: 3 };
    11	const PAGE_SIZE = 50;
    12	const ACCEPT = /^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp|tiff|bmp)$/;
    13	const $ = (sel) => document.querySelector(sel);
    14	
    15	const state = {
    16	  images: new Map(),      // fileKey (relative path or name, lower-cased) -> File
    17	  inputVersion: 0, builtVersion: -1, detailCache: new Map(),
    18	  csv: null,              // parsed CSV response
    19	  items: [],              // application rows (or one per image when there is no CSV)
    20	  unpaired: [],           // file names not attached to any row
    21	  extractions: new Map(), // file name -> extract response
    22	  decisions: new Map(),   // item key -> {decision, note}
    23	  running: false, abort: null, maxConcurrency: 3, concurrency: 3, successes: 0, inflight: 0, waiters: [], renderTimer: 0,
    24	  filter: "all", page: 0, expanded: null, times: [], startedAt: 0,
    25	};
    26	
    27	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers
    28	const norm = (name) => name.split(/[\\/]/).pop().toLowerCase();          // basename, lower-cased
    29	const stem = (name) => norm(name).replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/, "");
    30	const fileKey = (f) => (f.webkitRelativePath || f.name).toLowerCase();  // folder-aware identity
    31	function setStatus(text, busy = false) { const s = $("#batch-status"); s.textContent = text; s.classList.toggle("is-busy", busy); }
    32	function showError(msg) { const box = $("#batch-error"); box.querySelector("p").textContent = msg; box.hidden = !msg; }
    33	function seconds(ms) { return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`; }
    34	function percentile(arr, p) { if (!arr.length) return 0; const s = [...arr].sort((a, b) => a - b); return s[Math.min(s.length - 1, Math.floor(p * s.length))]; }
    35	
    36	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ intake
    37	function addImages(files) {
    38	  let added = 0;
    39	  for (const f of files) {
    40	    if (f.type && !ACCEPT.test(f.type)) continue;
    41	    if (f.size === 0 || f.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) continue;
    42	    const key = fileKey(f);
    43	    if (!state.images.has(key)) { state.images.set(key, f); added++; }
    44	  }
    45	  state.inputVersion++;
    46	  const byName = new Map();
    47	  for (const k of state.images.keys()) byName.set(norm(k), (byName.get(norm(k)) || 0) + 1);
    48	  const dupes = [...byName.entries()].filter(([, n]) => n > 1);
    49	  let msg = `${state.images.size} image${state.images.size === 1 ? "" : "s"} ready${added ? ` (${added} added)` : ""}.`;
    50	  if (dupes.length) msg += ` ${dupes.length} file name${dupes.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} appear${dupes.length === 1 ? "s" : ""} in more than one folder (${dupes.slice(0, 3).map(([n]) => n).join(", ")}${dupes.length > 3 ? ", …" : ""}); those are kept apart and cannot be paired by name alone.`;
    51	  $("#batch-image-count").textContent = msg;
    52	  updateStartButton();
    53	}
    54	
    55	async function setCsv(file) {
    56	  const fd = new FormData();
    57	  fd.append("file", file, file.name);
    58	  const box = $("#batch-csv-summary");
    59	  try {
    60	    const resp = await fetch("/api/v1/csv/parse", { method: "POST", body: fd });
    61	    const body = await resp.json();
    62	    if (!resp.ok) throw new ApiError(resp.status, body);
    63	    state.csv = body;
    64	    state.inputVersion++;
    65	    const bad = body.rows.filter((r) => r.errors.length);
    66	    box.replaceChildren(...[
    67	      el("p", { class: "text-bold margin-0", text: `${file.name}: ${body.rows.length} row${body.rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}${bad.length ? `, ${bad.length} with problems` : ""}.` }),
    68	      body.warnings.length ? el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled text-secondary-dark" }, body.warnings.map((w) => el("li", { text: w }))) : null,
    69	      bad.length ? el("details", {}, [el("summary", { text: "Rows with problems (they will be skipped)" }),
    70	        el("ul", { class: "usa-list" }, bad.slice(0, 20).map((r) => el("li", { text: `Row ${r.row_number}: ${r.errors.join("; ")}` })))]) : null,
    71	    ].filter(Boolean));
    72	  } catch (e) {
    73	    state.csv = null;
    74	    box.replaceChildren(el("p", { class: "text-secondary-dark text-bold", text: e.message || "Could not read the CSV." }));
    75	  }
    76	  updateStartButton();
    77	}
    78	
    79	function updateStartButton() {
    80	  $("#batch-start").disabled = state.running || state.images.size === 0;
    81	}
    82	
    83	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ pairing (explicit only)
    84	function buildItems() {
    85	  const files = new Map(state.images);       // copy; we remove as we attach
    86	  const items = [];
    87	  if (state.csv) {
    88	    for (const row of state.csv.rows) {
    89	      if (!row.application) continue;
    90	      const app = row.application;
    91	      const key = app.application_id || `row-${row.row_number}`;
    92	      const byName = (n) => [...files.keys()].filter((k) => norm(k) === norm(n));
    93	      let keys = [];
    94	      const missing = [];
    95	      for (const listed of row.images) {
    96	        const hits = byName(listed);
    97	        if (hits.length === 1) keys.push(hits[0]);
    98	        else missing.push(hits.length ? `${listed} (ambiguous: ${hits.length} files)` : listed);
    99	      }
   100	      let method = row.images.length ? (missing.length ? `listed in CSV (${missing.length} not found)` : "listed in CSV") : "none";
   101	      if (!row.images.length && app.application_id) {
   102	        const id = app.application_id.toLowerCase();
   103	        keys = [...files.keys()].filter((k) => { const st = stem(k); return st === id || st.startsWith(id + "_") || st.startsWith(id + "-") || st.startsWith(id + " "); });
   104	        method = keys.length ? "filename prefix" : "none";
   105	      }
   106	      const attached = keys.map((k) => files.get(k)).filter(Boolean);
   107	      for (const k of keys) files.delete(k);
   108	      items.push({ key, row: row.row_number, application: app, files: attached, missing, method,
   109	        result: null, error: null, ms: 0, status: attached.length ? "pending" : "no-images" });
   110	    }
   111	    state.unpaired = [...files.keys()];
   112	  } else {
   113	    for (const [n, f] of files) {
   114	      items.push({ key: n, row: null, application: null, files: [f], missing: [], method: "extract only", result: null, error: null, ms: 0, status: "pending" });
   115	    }
   116	    state.unpaired = [];
   117	  }
   118	  state.items = items;
   119	  state.builtVersion = state.inputVersion;
   120	  state.detailCache.clear();
   121	}
   122	
   123	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ processing
   124	// Request-level limiter: at most state.concurrency extract calls in flight, whatever the number of
   125	// applications being worked on. Adaptive: shrinks on 429, grows back slowly after successes.
   126	function acquireSlot() {
   127	  if (state.inflight < state.concurrency) { state.inflight++; return Promise.resolve(); }
   128	  return new Promise((resolve) => state.waiters.push(resolve));
   129	}
   130	function releaseSlot() {
   131	  if (state.waiters.length && state.inflight <= state.concurrency) { state.waiters.shift()(); return; }
   132	  state.inflight--;
   133	}
   134	
   135	async function extractOne(file, signal) {
   136	  const key = fileKey(file);
   137	  if (state.extractions.has(key)) return state.extractions.get(key);
   138	  let delay = 0;
   139	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++) {
   140	    if (delay) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delay)); delay = 0; } // back off without holding a slot
   141	    await acquireSlot();
   142	    try {
   143	      const t0 = performance.now();
   144	      const res = await extract([file], { batch: true, signal });
   145	      state.times.push(performance.now() - t0);
   146	      state.extractions.set(key, res);
   147	      state.successes++;
   148	      if (state.successes % 20 === 0 && state.concurrency < state.maxConcurrency) state.concurrency++;
   149	      return res;
   150	    } catch (e) {
   151	      if (e.name === "AbortError") throw e;
   152	      if (e instanceof ApiError && (e.status === 429 || e.status === 503)) {
   153	        state.concurrency = Math.max(1, state.concurrency - 1);
   154	        delay = (e.retryAfter || 1) * 1000 * (attempt + 1) + Math.random() * 500;
   155	        continue;
   156	      }
   157	      throw e;
   158	    } finally {
   159	      releaseSlot();
   160	    }
   161	  }
   162	  throw new ApiError(429, { message: "The service stayed busy; this item was skipped. Resume to try again." });
   163	}
   164	
   165	function linesForItem(item) {
   166	  const lines = [], images = [];
   167	  item.files.forEach((f, idx) => {
   168	    const ex = state.extractions.get(fileKey(f));
   169	    if (!ex) return;
   170	    for (const im of ex.images) images.push({ ...im, index: idx });
   171	    for (const ln of ex.lines) lines.push({ ...ln, image_index: idx });
   172	  });
   173	  return { lines, images };
   174	}
   175	
   176	async function processItem(item, signal) {
   177	  item.status = "working";
   178	  const t0 = performance.now();
   179	  try {
   180	    await Promise.all(item.files.map((f) => extractOne(f, signal)));
   181	    const { lines, images } = linesForItem(item);
   182	    if (item.application) {
   183	      const resp = await compare([{ item_id: item.key, application: item.application, lines, images }], { signal });
   184	      item.result = resp.results[0];
   185	      item.lines = lines;
   186	      item.images = images;
   187	    } else {
   188	      const ex = state.extractions.get(fileKey(item.files[0]));
   189	      item.result = null;
   190	      item.fields = ex.fields;
   191	      item.lines = lines;
   192	      item.images = images;
   193	    }
   194	    item.status = "done";
   195	  } catch (e) {
   196	    if (e.name === "AbortError") { item.status = "pending"; return; }
   197	    item.status = "error";
   198	    item.error = e instanceof ApiError ? `${e.message}${e.hint ? " " + e.hint : ""}` : "Network error.";
   199	  } finally {
   200	    item.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
   201	  }
   202	}
   203	
   204	function scheduleRender() {
   205	  if (state.renderTimer) return;
   206	  state.renderTimer = setTimeout(() => { state.renderTimer = 0; renderProgress(); renderTable(); }, 250);
   207	}
   208	
   209	async function runPool(items, signal) {
   210	  const queue = [...items];
   211	  let active = 0;
   212	  await new Promise((resolve) => {
   213	    const pump = () => {
   214	      if (signal.aborted && active === 0) return resolve();
   215	      while (!signal.aborted && active < state.maxConcurrency * 2 && queue.length) {
   216	        const item = queue.shift();
   217	        active++;
   218	        processItem(item, signal).finally(() => { active--; scheduleRender(); pump(); });
   219	      }
   220	      if (!queue.length && active === 0) resolve();
   221	    };
   222	    pump();
   223	  });
   224	}
   225	
   226	async function start() {
   227	  showError("");
   228	  if (!state.items.length || state.builtVersion !== state.inputVersion || state.items.every((i) => i.status === "done")) buildItems();
   229	  const pending = state.items.filter((i) => i.status === "pending" || i.status === "error");
   230	  if (!pending.length && !state.items.length) { showError("Add images first."); return; }
   231	  try { const h = await health(); state.maxConcurrency = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, h.max_concurrency)); } catch { state.maxConcurrency = 2; }
   232	  state.concurrency = state.maxConcurrency;
   233	  state.running = true;
   234	  state.abort = new AbortController();
   235	  state.startedAt = state.startedAt || performance.now();
   236	  $("#batch-start").hidden = true;
   237	  $("#batch-cancel").hidden = false;
   238	  $("#batch-progress").hidden = false;
   239	  $("#batch-results").hidden = false;
   240	  $("#batch-summary").hidden = false;
   241	  for (const it of pending) { it.status = "pending"; it.error = null; }
   242	  renderProgress(); renderTable(); renderUnpaired();
   243	  await runPool(pending, state.abort.signal);
   244	  state.running = false;
   245	  $("#batch-cancel").hidden = true;
   246	  $("#batch-start").hidden = false;
   247	  $("#batch-start").textContent = state.items.some((i) => i.status !== "done" && i.status !== "no-images") ? "Resume" : "Run again";
   248	  $("#batch-export").hidden = false;
   249	  updateStartButton();
   250	  renderProgress(); renderSummary(); renderTable();
   251	}
   252	
   253	function pause() { state.abort?.abort(); setStatus("Pausing after the images in progress…", true); }
   254	
   255	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ rendering
   256	function counts() {
   257	  const c = { total: state.items.length, done: 0, ready: 0, review: 0, issues: 0, unreadable: 0, errors: 0, noimages: 0, decided: 0 };
   258	  for (const it of state.items) {
   259	    if (it.status === "done") c.done++;
   260	    if (it.status === "error") c.errors++;
   261	    if (it.status === "no-images") c.noimages++;
   262	    const v = it.result?.verdict;
   263	    if (v === "ready_for_approval") c.ready++; else if (v === "needs_review") c.review++; else if (v === "issues_found") c.issues++; else if (v === "unreadable") c.unreadable++;
   264	    if (state.decisions.get(it.key)?.decision) c.decided++;
   265	  }
   266	  return c;
   267	}
   268	
   269	function renderProgress() {
   270	  const c = counts();
   271	  const finished = c.done + c.errors;
   272	  const workable = c.total - c.noimages;
   273	  const pct = workable ? Math.round((100 * finished) / workable) : 0;
   274	  const bar = $("#batch-progress .progress__bar");
   275	  bar.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(pct));
   276	  bar.querySelector("span").style.width = `${pct}%`;
   277	  const elapsed = performance.now() - state.startedAt;
   278	  const avgItem = finished ? elapsed / finished : 0;
   279	  const remaining = workable - finished;
   280	  const eta = state.running && finished ? ` · about ${seconds(avgItem * remaining / Math.max(1, state.concurrency))} left` : "";
   281	  $("#batch-progress .progress__text").textContent = `${finished} of ${workable} applications checked (${pct}%)${eta}`;
   282	  setStatus(state.running ? `Reading images, ${state.concurrency} at a time…` : finished ? `Done. ${finished} checked in ${seconds(elapsed)}.` : "", state.running);
   283	  renderSummary();
   284	}
   285	
   286	function renderSummary() {
   287	  const c = counts();
   288	  const tile = (num, lbl) => el("div", { class: "summary-tile" }, [el("div", { class: "num", text: String(num) }), el("div", { class: "lbl", text: lbl })]);
   289	  const perImage = state.times.length ? `${seconds(percentile(state.times, 0.5))} median · ${seconds(percentile(state.times, 0.95))} p95` : "—";
   290	  $("#batch-summary").replaceChildren(
   291	    tile(c.ready, "Ready for approval"), tile(c.review, "Need review"), tile(c.issues + c.unreadable, "Issues / unreadable"),
   292	    tile(c.errors, "Errors"), tile(state.unpaired.length, "Images not matched"), tile(`${c.decided}/${c.total}`, "Decided"),
   293	    el("div", { class: "summary-tile" }, [el("div", { class: "num", text: perImage.split(" · ")[0].replace(" median", "") }), el("div", { class: "lbl", text: state.times.length ? `per image, median (p95 ${perImage.split(" · ")[1].replace(" p95", "")})` : "per image" })]),
   294	  );
   295	}
   296	
   297	function visibleItems() {
   298	  const dec = (it) => state.decisions.get(it.key)?.decision;
   299	  let items = state.items.filter((it) => {
   300	    const v = it.result?.verdict;
   301	    if (state.filter === "attention") return it.status === "error" || it.status === "no-images" || v === "issues_found" || v === "needs_review" || v === "unreadable";
   302	    if (state.filter === "ready") return v === "ready_for_approval";
   303	    if (state.filter === "undecided") return it.status === "done" && !dec(it);
   304	    return true;
   305	  });
   306	  items = items.sort((a, b) => (SEVERITY[a.result?.verdict] ?? 4) - (SEVERITY[b.result?.verdict] ?? 4) || (a.row ?? 0) - (b.row ?? 0));
   307	  return items;
   308	}
   309	
   310	function issueList(item) {
   311	  if (!item.result) return null;
   312	  const probs = item.result.checks.filter((c) => c.status !== "match" && c.status !== "info" && c.status !== "not_checked").map((c) => `${c.label}: ${c.status.replace("_", " ")}`);
   313	  const w = item.result.warning;
   314	  if (!w.present) probs.push("Warning: missing"); else if (!w.exact) probs.push("Warning: wording not exact"); else if (w.anchor_caps !== "match") probs.push("Warning: heading not all caps");
   315	  return probs.length ? el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled issues" }, probs.map((p) => el("li", { text: p }))) : el("span", { class: "text-base", text: "All checks match" });
   316	}
   317	
   318	function decisionCell(item) {
   319	  const d = state.decisions.get(item.key) || {};
   320	  const btn = (val, label) => el("button", { type: "button", class: `usa-button usa-button--outline${d.decision === val ? " is-on" : ""}`, text: label,
   321	    "aria-pressed": d.decision === val ? "true" : "false",
   322	    onclick: () => { state.decisions.set(item.key, { ...d, decision: d.decision === val ? null : val }); renderTable(); renderSummary(); } });
   323	  const note = el("input", { type: "text", class: "note-input", placeholder: "Note (optional)", value: d.note || "", "aria-label": `Note for ${item.key}` });
   324	  note.addEventListener("change", () => state.decisions.set(item.key, { ...(state.decisions.get(item.key) || {}), note: note.value }));
   325	  return el("div", {}, [el("div", { class: "decision-btns" }, [btn("approve", "Approve"), btn("reject", "Reject"), btn("flag", "Flag")]), note]);
   326	}
   327	
   328	function renderTable() {
   329	  const items = visibleItems();
   330	  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(items.length / PAGE_SIZE));
   331	  state.page = Math.min(state.page, pages - 1);
   332	  const slice = items.slice(state.page * PAGE_SIZE, (state.page + 1) * PAGE_SIZE);
   333	  const rows = [];
   334	  for (const it of slice) {
   335	    const v = it.result?.verdict;
   336	    const statusCell = it.status === "working" ? el("span", { class: "status-line is-busy", text: "Reading" })
   337	      : it.status === "error" ? el("span", { class: "status-tag status-mismatch", text: "Error" }) : it.status === "no-images" ? statusTag("not_checked")
   338	      : it.status === "pending" ? el("span", { class: "text-base", text: "Waiting" })
   339	      : v ? el("span", { class: `status-tag status-${VERDICT_STATUS[v]}`, text: VERDICT_WORD[v] }) : statusTag("info");
   340	    const tr = el("tr", { class: it.status === "error" ? "is-error" : "" }, [
   341	      el("td", {}, statusCell),
   342	      el("td", {}, [el("div", { class: "app-id", text: it.application?.application_id || it.key }),
   343	        el("div", { class: "app-brand", text: it.application ? `${it.application.brand_name} · ${it.application.class_type}` : (it.fields?.largest_text ? `Read: ${it.fields.largest_text}` : "") })]),
   344	      el("td", {}, [el("div", { class: "files", text: it.files.map((f) => f.name).join(", ") || "none" }), el("div", { class: "files", text: it.method }),
   345	        it.missing && it.missing.length ? el("div", { class: "files text-secondary-dark", text: `Listed but not uploaded: ${it.missing.join(", ")}` }) : null]),
   346	      el("td", {}, it.status === "error" ? el("span", { class: "text-secondary-dark", text: it.error }) : it.status === "no-images" ? el("span", { text: "No images matched this row." })
   347	        : it.application ? issueList(it) : (it.fields ? el("ul", { class: "usa-list usa-list--unstyled issues" }, [
   348	          el("li", { text: `Alcohol: ${it.fields.alcohol_percent != null ? it.fields.alcohol_percent + "%" : "not read"}` }),
   349	          el("li", { text: `Net contents: ${it.fields.net_contents_ml.length ? it.fields.net_contents_ml.map((m) => m + " mL").join(", ") : "not read"}` }),
   350	          el("li", { text: `Warning: ${it.fields.warning_present ? "present" : "not found"}` })]) : null)),
   351	      el("td", {}, it.status === "done" && it.application ? decisionCell(it) : null),
   352	      el("td", {}, it.status === "done" ? el("button", { type: "button", class: "usa-button usa-button--unstyled", text: state.expanded === it.key ? "Hide" : "Details",
   353	        "aria-expanded": state.expanded === it.key ? "true" : "false", onclick: () => { state.expanded = state.expanded === it.key ? null : it.key; renderTable(); } }) : null),
   354	    ]);
   355	    rows.push(tr);
   356	    if (state.expanded === it.key && it.status === "done") {
   357	      let panel = state.detailCache.get(it.key);
   358	      if (!panel) {
   359	        panel = el("div", { class: "detail-panel" });
   360	        state.detailCache.set(it.key, panel);
   361	        renderDetail(panel, it);
   362	      }
   363	      rows.push(el("tr", { class: "detail-row" }, el("td", { colspan: "6" }, panel)));
   364	    }
   365	  }
   366	  $("#batch-table").replaceChildren(el("table", { class: "usa-table usa-table--stacked batch-table" }, [
   367	    el("thead", {}, el("tr", {}, ["Result", "Application", "Images", "What to look at", "Your decision", ""].map((h) => el("th", { scope: "col", text: h })))),
   368	    el("tbody", {}, rows.length ? rows : el("tr", {}, el("td", { colspan: "6", text: "Nothing to show for this filter." }))),
   369	  ]));
   370	  $("#pager-text").textContent = `Page ${state.page + 1} of ${pages} · ${items.length} application${items.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
   371	  $("#pager-prev").disabled = state.page === 0;
   372	  $("#pager-next").disabled = state.page >= pages - 1;
   373	}
   374	
   375	async function renderDetail(panel, it) {
   376	  if (!it.result) {
   377	    panel.replaceChildren(el("p", { text: "Read without application data. Lines:" }), el("ul", { class: "ocr-lines" }, it.lines.map((l) => el("li", { text: l.text }))));
   378	    return;
   379	  }
   380	  const checklist = el("div"), warning = el("div"), figures = el("div");
   381	  panel.replaceChildren(el("p", { class: "text-bold", text: it.result.summary }), el("div", { class: "grid-row grid-gap-4" }, [
   382	    el("div", { class: "desktop:grid-col-7" }, [checklist, el("h4", { text: "Government warning" }), warning]),
   383	    el("div", { class: "desktop:grid-col-5" }, figures)]));
   384	  const crops = await makeCrops(it.files, it.images, it.result.checks);
   385	  const overlays = renderFigures(figures, it.images, it.files, it.lines);
   386	  const select = (check) => { drawOverlays(overlays, it.result.checks, it.result.warning, check.id); panel.querySelectorAll(".checklist tbody tr").forEach((tr) => tr.classList.toggle("is-active", tr.dataset.check === check.id)); };
   387	  renderChecklist(checklist, it.result.checks, select, crops);
   388	  renderWarning(warning, it.result.warning, select);
   389	  drawOverlays(overlays, it.result.checks, it.result.warning, null);
   390	}
   391	
   392	function renderUnpaired() {
   393	  const sec = $("#batch-unpaired");
   394	  sec.hidden = state.unpaired.length === 0;
   395	  const list = $("#unpaired-list");
   396	  list.replaceChildren(...state.unpaired.map((name) => {
   397	    const f = state.images.get(name);
   398	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
   399	    const img = el("img", { src: url, alt: "" });
   400	    img.addEventListener("load", () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), { once: true });
   401	    const sel = el("select", { class: "usa-select", "aria-label": `Application for ${f.name}` }, [
   402	      el("option", { value: "", text: "Choose an application…" }),
   403	      ...state.items.filter((i) => i.application).map((i) => el("option", { value: i.key, text: `${i.key} · ${i.application.brand_name}` })),
   404	    ]);
   405	    sel.addEventListener("change", async () => {
   406	      const it = state.items.find((i) => i.key === sel.value);
   407	      if (!it) return;
   408	      it.files.push(f); it.method = "assigned by agent"; it.status = "pending"; state.detailCache.delete(it.key);
   409	      state.unpaired = state.unpaired.filter((n) => n !== name);
   410	      renderUnpaired();
   411	      const ac = new AbortController();
   412	      await processItem(it, ac.signal);
   413	      renderTable(); renderSummary();
   414	    });
   415	    return el("li", {}, [img, el("div", {}, [el("div", { class: "file-name", text: f.name })]), sel]);
   416	  }));
   417	}
   418	
   419	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ export
   420	function csvCell(v) {
   421	  let s = v == null ? "" : String(v);
   422	  if (/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(s)) s = "'" + s; // neutralize spreadsheet formulas
   423	  return `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
   424	}
   425	function exportCsv() {
   426	  const head = ["application_id", "brand_name", "class_type", "verdict", "summary", "brand_status", "class_status", "alcohol_status", "net_contents_status",
   427	    "bottler_status", "origin_status", "warning_present", "warning_exact", "warning_anchor_caps", "decision", "note", "images", "processing_ms", "checked_at"];
   428	  const lines = [head.map(csvCell).join(",")];
   429	  const st = (it, id) => it.result?.checks.find((c) => c.id === id)?.status || "";
   430	  for (const it of state.items) {
   431	    const d = state.decisions.get(it.key) || {};
   432	    const w = it.result?.warning;
   433	    lines.push([it.application?.application_id || it.key, it.application?.brand_name || it.fields?.largest_text || "", it.application?.class_type || "",
   434	      it.result?.verdict || it.status, it.result?.summary || it.error || "", st(it, "brand_name"), st(it, "class_type"), st(it, "alcohol_content"),
   435	      st(it, "net_contents"), st(it, "bottler"), st(it, "country_of_origin"), w ? w.present : "", w ? w.exact : "", w ? w.anchor_caps : "",
   436	      d.decision || "", d.note || "", it.files.map((f) => f.name).join(";"), it.ms, new Date().toISOString()].map(csvCell).join(","));
   437	  }
   438	  const blob = new Blob(["﻿" + lines.join("\r\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
   439	  const a = el("a", { href: URL.createObjectURL(blob), download: `label-check-batch-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace(/[:T]/g, "-")}.csv` });
   440	  document.body.append(a); a.click(); a.remove();
   441	}
   442	
   443	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ demo batch
   444	async function loadDemo() {
   445	  setStatus("Loading demo batch…", true);
   446	  const names = ["APP-001_front.png", "APP-001_back.png", "APP-002_front.png", "APP-002_back.png", "APP-003_front.png", "APP-003_back.png",
   447	    "APP-005_front.png", "APP-005_back.png", "APP-007_front.png", "APP-007_back.png"];
   448	  const files = [];
   449	  for (const n of names) { const b = await (await fetch(`/static/samples/batch/${n}`)).blob(); files.push(new File([b], n, { type: "image/png" })); }
   450	  state.images = new Map(); state.items = []; state.extractions = new Map(); state.decisions = new Map(); state.times = []; state.startedAt = 0;
   451	  addImages(files);
   452	  const csvBlob = await (await fetch("/static/samples/batch/applications.csv")).blob();
   453	  await setCsv(new File([csvBlob], "applications.csv", { type: "text/csv" }));
   454	  setStatus("");
   455	  await start();
   456	}
   457	
   458	// ------------------------------------------------------------------ boot
   459	function boot() {
   460	  const dz = $("#batch-dropzone");
   461	  if (!dz) return;
   462	  ["dragenter", "dragover"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.add("is-dragover"); }));
   463	  ["dragleave", "drop"].forEach((t) => dz.addEventListener(t, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.remove("is-dragover"); }));
   464	  dz.addEventListener("drop", (e) => addImages(e.dataTransfer.files));
   465	  $("#batch-files").addEventListener("change", (e) => { addImages(e.target.files); e.target.value = ""; });
   466	  $("#batch-folder").addEventListener("change", (e) => { addImages(e.target.files); e.target.value = ""; });
   467	  $("#batch-csv").addEventListener("change", (e) => { if (e.target.files[0]) setCsv(e.target.files[0]); e.target.value = ""; });
   468	  $("#batch-start").addEventListener("click", start);
   469	  $("#batch-cancel").addEventListener("click", pause);
   470	  $("#batch-export").addEventListener("click", exportCsv);
   471	  $("#batch-demo").addEventListener("click", loadDemo);
   472	  document.querySelectorAll("[data-filter]").forEach((b) => b.addEventListener("click", () => {
   473	    state.filter = b.dataset.filter; state.page = 0;
   474	    document.querySelectorAll("[data-filter]").forEach((x) => x.classList.toggle("is-on", x === b));
   475	    renderTable();
   476	  }));
   477	  $("#pager-prev").addEventListener("click", () => { state.page--; renderTable(); });
   478	  $("#pager-next").addEventListener("click", () => { state.page++; renderTable(); });
   479	  window.addEventListener("beforeunload", (e) => { if (state.items.some((i) => i.status === "done")) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ""; } });
   480	}
   481	
   482	boot();

   237	    <!-- ============================================================ About -->
   238	    <section id="view-about" class="usa-section padding-top-4" data-view-panel="about" hidden>
   239	      <div class="grid-container usa-prose">
   240	        <h1 class="margin-top-0">About this tool</h1>
   241	        <h2>What it does</h2>
   242	        <p>It reads the text on label images with an on-device text-recognition model, compares what it read with what the application says, checks the government warning statement word for word against 27 CFR 16.21, and shows you exactly where on the label it found each item. It works in a few seconds and makes no calls to outside services.</p>
   243	        <h2>What it does not do</h2>
   244	        <ul>
   245	          <li>It does not approve or reject applications. Every result is a recommendation for a compliance agent.</li>
   246	          <li>It does not store images, application data, or results. Refreshing the page clears everything.</li>
   247	          <li>It does not check permits, formulas, class/type validity, age statements or laboratory tolerances.</li>
   248	          <li>It does not measure physical type size or bold type from a photograph reliably; those items are marked for you to confirm.</li>
   249	        </ul>
   250	        <h2>How it decides</h2>
   251	        <p><strong>Match</strong> means the label says the same thing, allowing for letter case and accents. <strong>Needs review</strong> means it is close but you should look, which is usually OCR noise. <strong>Mismatch</strong> and <strong>Not found</strong> mean the label disagrees or the item could not be read. For the warning statement, only an exact match passes.</p>
   252	        <h2>Accessibility</h2>
   253	        <p>Built with the U.S. Web Design System. Target: WCAG 2.1 AA and Section 508. Every status is shown as an icon, a word and a color, never a color alone. The page works with keyboard only, at 200% zoom, and in Windows High Contrast Mode. If something does not work for you, please say so in the project repository.</p>
   254	        <h2>For engineers</h2>
   255	        <p>API description: <a href="/api/v1/openapi.json">/api/v1/openapi.json</a>. Health and capacity: <a href="/api/v1/health">/api/v1/health</a>. Source code and documentation: <a id="repo-link" href="#">repository</a>.</p>
   256	      </div>
   257	    </section>

     1	// Results rendering: verdict, checklist, warning report, label figures with highlight overlays,
     2	// and evidence crops cut from the original image in the browser. All user/OCR text goes through
     3	// textContent; nothing is ever inserted as HTML.
     4	
     5	const STATUS = {
     6	  match: { word: "Match", icon: "check_circle" },
     7	  needs_review: { word: "Needs review", icon: "warning" },
     8	  mismatch: { word: "Mismatch", icon: "cancel" },
     9	  not_found: { word: "Not found", icon: "error" },
    10	  not_checked: { word: "Not checked", icon: "remove_circle" },
    11	  info: { word: "Info", icon: "info" },
    12	};
    13	
    14	const VERDICT = {
    15	  ready_for_approval: { heading: "Ready for your approval", kind: "success" },
    16	  needs_review: { heading: "Please confirm the items marked", kind: "warning" },
    17	  issues_found: { heading: "Issues found", kind: "error" },
    18	  unreadable: { heading: "Could not read the images", kind: "error" },
    19	};

    21	/** Tiny DOM helper: el("div", {class: "x", text: "hi"}, [children]) */
    22	export function el(tag, attrs = {}, children = []) {
    23	  const node = document.createElement(tag);
    24	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) {
    25	    if (v === null || v === undefined || v === false) continue;
    26	    if (k === "text") node.textContent = String(v);
    27	    else if (k === "class") node.className = v;
    28	    else if (k === "dataset") Object.assign(node.dataset, v);
    29	    else if (k.startsWith("on") && typeof v === "function") node.addEventListener(k.slice(2), v);
    30	    else node.setAttribute(k, v === true ? "" : String(v));
    31	  }
    32	  for (const c of [].concat(children)) {
    33	    if (c === null || c === undefined) continue;
    34	    node.append(typeof c === "string" ? document.createTextNode(c) : c);
    35	  }
    36	  return node;
    37	}
    38	
    39	export function icon(name, cls = "usa-icon") {
    40	  const svg = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "svg");
    41	  svg.setAttribute("class", cls);
    42	  svg.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    43	  svg.setAttribute("focusable", "false");
    44	  svg.setAttribute("role", "img");
    45	  const use = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "use");
    46	  use.setAttribute("href", `#${name}`);
    47	  svg.append(use);
    48	  return svg;
    49	}
    50	
    51	export function statusTag(status) {
    52	  const s = STATUS[status] || STATUS.not_checked;
    53	  return el("span", { class: `status-tag status-${status}` }, [icon(s.icon), s.word]);
    54	}

    56	function seconds(ms) { return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`; }
    57	
    58	export function renderVerdict(container, result) {
    59	  const v = VERDICT[result.verdict] || VERDICT.needs_review;
    60	  container.replaceChildren(
    61	    el("div", { class: `usa-alert usa-alert--${v.kind} verdict`, role: "status" }, [
    62	      el("div", { class: "usa-alert__body" }, [
    63	        el("h2", { class: "usa-alert__heading", text: v.heading }),
    64	        el("p", { class: "usa-alert__text", text: result.summary }),
    65	        el("p", { class: "usa-alert__text timing" }, [
    66	          `Checked in ${seconds(result.timing.total_ms)}`,
    67	          el("span", { class: "text-normal" }, ` (target: under 5 seconds). ${result.images.length} image${result.images.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} read.`),
    68	        ]),
    69	      ]),
    70	    ]),
    71	  );
    72	}
